[SYSTEM]
You are an RTL (Register Transfer Level) design agent. Your task is to create a Verilog/SystemVerilog design that satisfies the given specification, is functionally correct, and has minimal cost (area_delay_product).

**Cost metric `area_delay_product`:** area × achieved critical-path delay — the classic area-delay product. Does NOT include simulation cycle count (contrast with the 'area_runtime_product' metric). Cost is measured on your design alone. The testbench and everything it owns (checking logic, the external memories, etc.) is not synthesized and contributes nothing to area, delay, power, or energy.

## Specification
Full 8x8 2-D IDCT of a baseline JPEG decoder (ultraembedded/core_jpeg), module
name `jpeg_idct_2d`: the row pass (jpeg_idct_x, OUT_SHIFT=11), a double-buffered
corner-turn (jpeg_idct_transpose with four 32x32 dual-port RAMs), and the column
pass (jpeg_idct_y, OUT_SHIFT=15), composed exactly as in the original decoder
(downstream ready tied high; the row pass accepts no backpressure). Each 1-D
pass is the factorized (AAN-style) algorithm with cosine constants scaled by
4096 (C1..C7 = 4017, 3784, 3406, 2896, 2276, 1567, 799), time-sharing 5
multipliers over an 8-beat schedule. ALL arithmetic is evaluated in 32-bit
signed context with wrap-around; outputs of each pass are arithmetic-shifted
right (11 then 15); the 1/sqrt(2) odd-part scaling is `(x * 181) / 256` where
`*` wraps at 32 bits and `/` is SIGNED division truncating toward zero. This
exact fixed-point behavior is what the golden vectors encode.

Interface (clocked, sync active-high reset `rst`; no backpressure):
  inputs : clk, rst, img_start_i (1-cycle pulse resets the output streamers),
           img_end_i (unused), inport_valid_i, inport_idx_i[2:0],
           inport_data0..3_i[15:0]
  outputs: outport_valid_o, outport_data_o[31:0], outport_idx_o[5:0]

Input protocol (the upstream block serializer's FIXED schedule, reproduced
exactly by the testbench): per row of 8 coefficients c0..c7 (16-bit signed),
8 back-to-back valid beats with idx = 0..7; the four data lanes carry the EVEN
set (c0,c2,c4,c6) on beats idx in {0,2,5,7} and the ODD set (c1,c3,c5,c7) on
beats idx in {1,3,4,6}. 8 rows back-to-back form a 64-beat block; between
blocks there are >= 2 idle cycles (valid=0, idx=0, data=0). Pipeline registers
advance every clock regardless of valid — designs must be robust to the gap
pattern exactly as the original is.

Output (fixed schedule): 64 values per block streamed on outport_valid_o; the
k-th streamed value of a block carries outport_idx_o = {k[2:0], k[5:3]} (the
column pass emits column-major; the idx label gives the ROW-MAJOR pixel
position) and must match the expected output at that position. The stream of a
block overlaps the next block's input beats (the transpose double-buffers one
full block).

A working starting point is provided: `starting_point.v` contains the
ORIGINAL core_jpeg modules (Apache 2.0), unmodified, under a thin composition
top. It passes all golden vectors (they were generated from it — the original
RTL is the correctness oracle). Study it, then optimize while preserving its
exact behavior. Sibling benchmark: `jpeg_idct_2d_spire` (identical tb.sv +
vectors.dat, bit-exact SpireHDL starting point). The 1-D row pass alone is
also available as the `jpeg_idct_x_*` benchmarks.

NOTE on memories: the cost-evaluation synthesis flow targets a standard-cell
library with no RAM macros, so every behavioral memory in this design (the
transpose buffers, block_out, etc.) is inferred as a register array plus mux
decode REGARDLESS of coding style. Optimizing that decode/mux logic is fair
game; instantiating a hard RAM macro is not possible in this flow.

## Tools
- create_file: Create a new file in your working directory
- replace_file: Replace the entire contents of an existing file
- apply_diff: Apply a unified diff to modify a file
- edit_file: Edit a file by replacing an exact string match with new content. Provide old_string (must match exactly once) and new_string.
- ls: List files in your working directory
- read_file: Read the contents of a file
- run_evaluation(filename[, target_delay]): Run evaluation on the given design file. Returns correctness (Verilator simulation) + cost (Yosys area_delay_product). The filename must be your main design file (e.g. 'design.sv'). Optionally pass target_delay (ps) to override the synthesis timing constraint.

## Strategy
1. Lay out an action plan. Try to cover a diverse set of approaches in your plan to increase the chances of finding a good solution within the step limit. Also once you find a new best solution, explore close solutions. Trade off exploration with exploitation.
2. First, create a simple, straightforward design that is functionally correct. Focus on getting 100% correctness first.
3. Run evaluation to confirm correctness and get the initial cost (area_delay_product).
4. ONLY after achieving 100% correctness, try to optimize the design to reduce the area_delay_product.
5. After each optimization, run evaluation to verify correctness is maintained and check the new cost.
6. If an optimization breaks correctness, revert and try a different approach.
7. Keep iterating until you run out of steps — use every step to try improvements. The run ends automatically at the step limit; your best passing design is kept.

In general, be creative and take risk. At least after you have a correct design, try bold optimizations to reduce area_delay_product, even if they might break correctness. You can always revert if it doesn't work.
The best results you obtain which is 100% correctness counts.
Try to beat at least the straightforward implementation.

Running evaluation is crucial after every change to ensure correctness and track cost. Do not skip evaluations.

You may do some 'thinking' alongside with your tool calling responses to explain your reasoning, but always remember to call a tool in every response.

## Important
- Always call a tool in every response.
- You have a maximum of **60 steps** (LLM turns). Budget them wisely: get a correct design first, then optimise. You may batch tool calls in one response --- at most 8, and only one `run_evaluation`.
- The best result is the one with lowest cost among designs that pass 100% of testbench checks.
- Use different filenames for different design variants so you can retrieve them later.
- If context files (e.g. component source files) are in your workspace, you can modify them directly using edit_file or replace_file to optimize the design. Make targeted, incremental changes rather than rewriting files from scratch — small edits are less likely to introduce bugs.
- Write clean, synthesizable RTL code.
- You can pass `target_delay` (ps) to `run_evaluation` to try different synthesis timing constraints; experimenting with this may improve PPA results (lower target delay may reduce delay, higher target delay may reduce area/transistor count). target_delay must be > 0.
- Do not spend too many steps sweeping `target_delay` — basically if you want to minimize delay choose a low value,if you want to minimize area/transistor count choose a high value. That's it.A few targeted experiments are more productive than an exhaustive sweep.

## Delay Optimization Tips
- `target_delay` trades area against delay (a lower value pressures toward faster logic, a higher value lets Yosys re-map to smaller cells). Do not overly iterate on different target delays; often the RTL structure is the real lever, target_delay is only a minor secondary knob.
- After getting a correct design, try different accumulation orderings (sequential vs. tree) and compare results — Yosys synthesis can produce noticeably different delays (~10 ps) from structurally similar descriptions.
- For designs dominated by a multiply-accumulate chain, the critical path is the multiplier followed by the adder tree. Reducing the adder depth or splitting into parallel partial sums can help.

## Reference Implementations
The following are reference implementations for inspiration. Study them for patterns and techniques, but do not copy them directly — adapt ideas to your specific design.

### Parallel-prefix adder (ripple-carry / carry-lookahead) (Add.sv)
```systemverilog
// Copyright 2024 ETH Zurich and University of Bologna.
// Solderpad Hardware License, Version 0.51, see LICENSE for details.
// SPDX-License-Identifier: SHL-0.51
//
// Based on the work by Reto Zimmermann 1998 - ETH Zürich
// Originally written in VHDL, available under: 
// https://iis-people.ee.ethz.ch/~zimmi/arith_lib.html#library
//
// Authors:
// - Thomas Benz <tbenz@iis.ee.ethz.ch>
// - Philippe Sauter <phsauter@iis.ee.ethz.ch>
// - Paul Scheffler <paulsc@iis.ee.ethz.ch>
//
// Description :
// Binary adder using ripple-carry or parallel-prefix carry-lookahead logic.
// S = A+B

module Add #(
	parameter int              width = 8,             // word width
	parameter lau_pkg::speed_e speed = lau_pkg::FAST  // performance parameter
) (
	input  logic [width-1:0] A,  // operands
	input  logic [width-1:0] B,
	output logic [width-1:0] S   // sum
);

	// Function: Binary adder using parallel-prefix carry-lookahead logic.

	logic [width-1:0] GI, PI;  // prefix gen./prop. in
	logic [width-1:0] GO, PO;  // prefix gen./prop. out
	logic [width-1:0] PT;  // adder propagate temp

	// Internal signals for unsigned operands
	logic [width-1:0] Auns, Buns, Suns;

	// default ripple-carry adder as slow implementation
	if (speed == lau_pkg::SLOW) begin
		// type conversion: std_logic_vector -> unsigned
		assign Auns = A;
		assign Buns = B;

		// addition
		assign Suns = Auns + Buns;

		// type conversion: unsigned -> std_logic_vector
		assign S = Suns;
	end else begin
		// parallel-prefix adders as medium and fast implementations

		// calculate prefix input generate/propagate signals
		assign GI = A & B;
		assign PI = A | B;
		// calculate adder propagate signals (PT = A xor B)
		assign PT = ~GI & PI;

		// calculate prefix output generate/propagate signals
		PrefixAndOr #(
			.width(width),
			.speed(speed)
		) prefix (
			.GI(GI),
			.PI(PI),
			.GO(GO),
			.PO(PO)
		);

		// calculate sum bits
		assign S = PT ^ {GO[width-2:0], 1'b0};
	end
endmodule



module behavioural_Add #(
	parameter int              width = 8,             // word width
	parameter lau_pkg::speed_e speed = lau_pkg::FAST  // performance parameter
) (
	input  logic [width-1:0] A,  // operands
	input  logic [width-1:0] B,
	output logic [width-1:0] S   // sum
);
	assign S = A + B;
endmodule
```

### Prefix AND-OR structures (Sklansky, Brent-Kung, serial) (PrefixAndOr.sv)
```systemverilog
// Copyright 2024 ETH Zurich and University of Bologna.
// Solderpad Hardware License, Version 0.51, see LICENSE for details.
// SPDX-License-Identifier: SHL-0.51
//
// Based on the work by Reto Zimmermann 1998 - ETH Zürich
// Originally written in VHDL, available under: 
// https://iis-people.ee.ethz.ch/~zimmi/arith_lib.html#library
//
// Authors:
// - Thomas Benz <tbenz@iis.ee.ethz.ch>
// - Philippe Sauter <phsauter@iis.ee.ethz.ch>
// - Paul Scheffler <paulsc@iis.ee.ethz.ch>
//
// Description :
// Prefix structures of different depth (i.e. speed) for carry calculation
// in binary adders. Compute in m levels new generate/propagate signal pairs
// for always larger groups of bits. Generate signals of the last level correspond
// to carries in binary addition. Basic logic operations: AND-OR for generate
// signals, AND for propagate signals.

module PrefixAndOr #(
	parameter int              width = 8,             // word width
	parameter lau_pkg::speed_e speed = lau_pkg::FAST  // performance parameter
) (
	input  logic [width-1:0] GI,  // gen./prop. in
	input  logic [width-1:0] PI,  // gen./prop. in
	output logic [width-1:0] GO,  // gen./prop. out
	output logic [width-1:0] PO   // gen./prop. out
);

	// Constants
	localparam int n = width;  // prefix structure width
	localparam int m = $clog2(width);  // prefix structure depth

	// Sklansky parallel-prefix carry-lookahead structure
	if (speed == lau_pkg::FAST) begin : fastPrefix
		logic [(m+1)*n-1:0] GT, PT;  // gen./prop. temp
			assign GT[n-1:0] = GI;
			assign PT[n-1:0] = PI;
			for (genvar l = 1; l <= m; l++) begin : levels
				for (genvar k = 0; k < 2 ** (m - l); k++) begin : groups
					for (genvar i = 0; i < 2 ** (l - 1); i++) begin : bits
						// pass prop and gen to following nodes
						if ((k * 2 ** l + i) < n) begin : white
							assign GT[l*n+k*2**l+i] = GT[(l-1)*n+k*2**l+i];
							assign PT[l*n+k*2**l+i] = PT[(l-1)*n+k*2**l+i];
						end
						// calculate new propagate and generate
						if ((k * 2 ** l + 2 ** (l - 1) + i) < n) begin : black
							assign GT[l*n + k*2**l + 2**(l-1) + i] = 
												GT[(l-1)*n + k*2**l + 2**(l-1) + i]
											  | (  PT[(l-1)*n + k*2**l + 2**(l-1) + i]
												 & GT[(l-1)*n + k*2**l + 2**(l-1) - 1] );
							assign PT[l*n + k*2**l + 2**(l-1) + i] = 
													PT[(l-1)*n + k*2**l + 2**(l-1) + i]
												  & PT[(l-1)*n + k*2**l + 2**(l-1) - 1];
						end
					end
				end
			end
			assign GO = GT[(m+1)*n-1 : m*n];
			assign PO = PT[(m+1)*n-1 : m*n];
	end

	// Brent-Kung parallel-prefix carry-lookahead structure
	if (speed == lau_pkg::MEDIUM) begin : mediumPrefix
		logic [(2*m)*n -1:0] GT, PT;  // gen./prop. temp
		assign GT[n-1:0] = GI;
		assign PT[n-1:0] = PI;

		for (genvar l = 1; l <= m; l++) begin : levels1
			for (genvar k = 0; k < 2**(m-l); k++) begin : groups
				for (genvar i = 0; i < 2**l -1; i++) begin : bits
					if ((k* 2**l +i) < n) begin : white
						assign GT[l*n + k* 2**l +i] = GT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**l +i];
						assign PT[l*n + k* 2**l +i] = PT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**l +i];
					end // white
				end // bits
				if ((k* 2**l + 2**l -1) < n) begin : black
					assign GT[l*n + k* 2**l + 2**l -1] =
								GT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**l + 2**l - 1] |
							  | (  PT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**l + 2**l     -1] 
							     & GT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**l + 2**(l-1) -1]);
					assign PT[l*n + k* 2**l + 2**l -1] =
								PT[(l-1)*n + k*2**l + 2**l     -1] 
							  & PT[(l-1)*n + k*2**l + 2**(l-1) -1];
				end // black
			end
		end // level1
		for (genvar l = m +1; l < 2*m; l++) begin : levels2
			for (genvar i = 0; i < 2**(2*m -l); i++) begin : bits
				if (i < n) begin : white
					assign GT[l*n +i] = GT[(l-1)*n +i];
					assign PT[l*n +i] = PT[(l-1)*n +i];
				end // white
			end // bits
			for (genvar k = 1; k < 2**(l-m); k++) begin : groups
				if (l < 2*m -1) begin : empty
					for (genvar i = 0; i < 2**(2*m -l -1) -1; i++) begin : bits
						if ((k* 2**(2*m -l) +i) < n) begin : white
							assign GT[l*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) +i] = GT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) +i];
							assign PT[l*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) +i] = PT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) +i];
						end // white
					end
				end // empty
				if ((k* 2**(2*m -l) + 2**(2*m -l -1) -1) < n) begin : black
					assign GT[l*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) + 2**(2*m -l-1) -1] = 
								GT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**(2*m-l) + 2**(2*m -l-1) -1]
							  | (  PT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**(2*m-l) + 2**(2*m-l-1) -1] 
								 & GT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**(2*m-l) -1] );
					assign PT[l*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) + 2**(2*m -l-1) -1] = 
								PT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) + 2**(2*m -l-1) -1]
							  & PT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) -1];
				end // black
				for (genvar i = 2**(2*m -l-1); i < 2**(2*m -l); i++) begin : bits
					if ((k* 2**(2*m -l) +i) < n) begin : white
						assign GT[l*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) +i] = GT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) +i];
						assign PT[l*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) +i] = PT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) +i];
					end // white
				end
			end
		end // level2
		assign GO = GT[2*m*n -1 : (2*m -1) * n];
		assign PO = PT[2*m*n -1 : (2*m -1) * n];
	end  // Serial-prefix carry-lookahead structure
	else if (speed == lau_pkg::SLOW) begin : slowPrefix
		logic [n-1:0] GT, PT;  // gen./prop. temp
		assign GT[0] = GI[0];
		assign PT[0] = PI[0];
		
		for (genvar i = 1; i < n; i++) begin : bits
			assign GT[i] = GI[i] | (PI[i] & GT[i-1]);
			assign PT[i] = PI[i] & PT[i-1];
		end
		assign GO = GT;
		assign PO = PT;
	end

endmodule

```

wire signed [31:0] block_in_0_1 = {{16{inport_data0_i[15]}}, inport_data0_i};
wire signed [31:0] block_in_2_3 = {{16{inport_data1_i[15]}}, inport_data1_i};
wire signed [31:0] block_in_4_5 = {{16{inport_data2_i[15]}}, inport_data2_i};
wire signed [31:0] block_in_6_7 = {{16{inport_data3_i[15]}}, inport_data3_i};

//-----------------------------------------------------------------
// IDCT
//-----------------------------------------------------------------
reg signed [31:0] i0;
reg signed [31:0] mul0_a;
reg signed [31:0] mul0_b;
reg signed [31:0] mul1_a;
reg signed [31:0] mul1_b;
reg signed [31:0] mul2_a;
reg signed [31:0] mul2_b;
reg signed [31:0] mul3_a;
reg signed [31:0] mul3_b;
reg signed [31:0] mul4_a;
reg signed [31:0] mul4_b;

always @ (posedge clk )
if (rst)
begin
    i0     <= 32'b0;
    mul0_a <= 32'b0;
    mul0_b <= 32'b0;
    mul1_a <= 32'b0;
    mul1_b <= 32'b0;
    mul2_a <= 32'b0;
    mul2_b <= 32'b0;
    mul3_a <= 32'b0;
    mul3_b <= 32'b0;
    mul4_a <= 32'b0;
    mul4_b <= 32'b0;
end
else
begin
    /* verilator lint_off WIDTH */
    case (inport_idx_i)
    3'd0:
    begin
        i0     <= block_in_0_1 + block_in_4_5;
        mul0_a <= block_in_2_3;
        mul0_b <= C2_16;
        mul1_a <= block_in_6_7;
        mul1_b <= C6_16;
    end
    3'd1:
    begin
        mul0_a <= block_in_0_1;
        mul0_b <= C1_16;
        mul1_a <= block_in_6_7;
        mul1_b <= C7_16;
        mul2_a <= block_in_4_5;
        mul2_b <= C5_16;
        mul3_a <= block_in_2_3;
        mul3_b <= C3_16;
        mul4_a <= i0;
        mul4_b <= C4_16;
    end
    3'd2:
    begin
        i0     <= block_in_0_1 - block_in_4_5;
    end
    3'd3:
    begin
        mul0_a <= block_in_0_1;
        mul0_b <= C7_16;
        mul1_a <= block_in_6_7;
        mul1_b <= C1_16;
        mul2_a <= block_in_4_5;
        mul2_b <= C3_16;
        mul3_a <= block_in_2_3;
        mul3_b <= C5_16;
    end
    3'd4:
    begin
        mul0_a <= block_in_0_1;
        mul0_b <= C7_16;
        mul1_a <= block_in_6_7;
        mul1_b <= C1_16;
        mul2_a <= block_in_4_5;
        mul2_b <= C3_16;
        mul3_a <= block_in_2_3;
        mul3_b <= C5_16;
    end
    3'd5:
    begin
        mul0_a <= block_in_2_3;
        mul0_b <= C6_16;
        mul1_a <= block_in_6_7;
        mul1_b <= C2_16;
        mul4_a <= i0;
        mul4_b <= C4_16;
    end
    default:
        ;
    endcase
    /* verilator lint_on WIDTH */
end

reg signed [31:0] mul0_q;
reg signed [31:0] mul1_q;
reg signed [31:0] mul2_q;
reg signed [31:0] mul3_q;
reg signed [31:0] mul4_q;

always @ (posedge clk )
if (rst)
begin
    mul0_q <= 32'b0;
    mul1_q <= 32'b0;
    mul2_q <= 32'b0;
    mul3_q <= 32'b0;
    mul4_q <= 32'b0;
end
else
begin
    mul0_q <= mul0_a * mul0_b;
    mul1_q <= mul1_a * mul1_b;
    mul2_q <= mul2_a * mul2_b;
    mul3_q <= mul3_a * mul3_b;
    mul4_q <= mul4_a * mul4_b;
end

reg signed [31:0] mul0;
reg signed [31:0] mul1;
reg signed [31:0] mul2;
reg signed [31:0] mul3;
reg signed [31:0] mul4;

always @ (posedge clk )
if (rst)
begin
    mul0 <= 32'b0;
    mul1 <= 32'b0;
    mul2 <= 32'b0;
    mul3 <= 32'b0;
    mul4 <= 32'b0;
end
else
begin
    mul0 <= mul0_q;
    mul1 <= mul1_q;
    mul2 <= mul2_q;
    mul3 <= mul3_q;
    mul4 <= mul4_q;
end

reg        out_stg0_valid_q;
reg [2:0]  out_stg0_idx_q;

always @ (posedge clk )
if (rst)
begin
    out_stg0_valid_q <= 1'b0;
    out_stg0_idx_q   <= 3'b0;
end
else
begin
    out_stg0_valid_q <= inport_valid_i;
    out_stg0_idx_q   <= inport_idx_i;
end

reg        out_stg1_valid_q;
reg [2:0]  out_stg1_idx_q;

always @ (posedge clk )
if (rst)
begin
    out_stg1_valid_q <= 1'b0;
    out_stg1_idx_q   <= 3'b0;
end
else
begin
    out_stg1_valid_q <= out_stg0_valid_q;
    out_stg1_idx_q   <= out_stg0_idx_q;
end

reg        out_stg2_valid_q;
reg [2:0]  out_stg2_idx_q;

always @ (posedge clk )
if (rst)
begin
    out_stg2_valid_q <= 1'b0;
    out_stg2_idx_q   <= 3'b0;
end
else
begin
    out_stg2_valid_q <= out_stg1_valid_q;
    out_stg2_idx_q   <= out_stg1_idx_q;
end

reg signed [31:0] o_s5;
reg signed [31:0] o_s6;
reg signed [31:0] o_s7;
reg signed [31:0] o_t0;
reg signed [31:0] o_t1;
reg signed [31:0] o_t2;
reg signed [31:0] o_t3;
reg signed [31:0] o_t4;
reg signed [31:0] o_t5;
reg signed [31:0] o_t6;
reg signed [31:0] o_t7;
reg signed [31:0] o_t6_5;
reg signed [31:0] o_t5_6;

always @ (posedge clk )
if (rst)
begin
    o_s5   <= 32'b0;
    o_s6   <= 32'b0;
    o_s7   <= 32'b0;
    o_t0   <= 32'b0;
    o_t1   <= 32'b0;
    o_t2   <= 32'b0;
    o_t3   <= 32'b0;
    o_t4   <= 32'b0;
    o_t5   <= 32'b0;
    o_t6   <= 32'b0;
    o_t7   <= 32'b0;
    o_t6_5 <= 32'b0;
    o_t5_6 <= 32'b0;
end
else
begin
    case (out_stg2_idx_q)
    3'd0:
    begin
        o_t3 <= mul0 + mul1; // s3
    end
    3'd1:
    begin
        o_s7 <= mul0 + mul1;
        o_s6 <= mul2 + mul3;
        o_t0 <= mul4;        // s0
    end
    3'd2:
    begin
        o_t0 <= o_t0 + o_t3; // t0
        o_t3 <= o_t0 - o_t3; // t3
        o_t7 <= o_s6 + o_s7;
    end
    3'd3:
    begin
        o_t4 <= (mul0 - mul1) + (mul2 - mul3);
    end
    3'd4:
    begin
        o_t0 <= mul0 - mul1; // s4
        o_s5 <= mul2 - mul3;    
    end
    3'd5:
    begin
        o_t3 <= mul0 - mul1; // s2
        o_t4 <= mul4; // s1
        o_t5 <= o_t0 - o_s5;
        o_t6 <= o_s7 - o_s6;
    end
    3'd6:
    begin
        o_t1 <= o_t4 + o_t3;
        o_t2 <= o_t4 - o_t3;
        o_t6_5 <= o_t6 - o_t5;
        o_t5_6 <= o_t5 + o_t6;
    end
    default:
    begin
        o_s5 <= (o_t6_5 * 181) / 256; // 1/sqrt(2)
        o_s6 <= (o_t5_6 * 181) / 256; // 1/sqrt(2)
    end
    endcase
end

reg        out_stg3_valid_q;
reg [2:0]  out_stg3_idx_q;

always @ (posedge clk )
if (rst)
begin
    out_stg3_valid_q <= 1'b0;
    out_stg3_idx_q   <= 3'b0;
end
else
begin
    out_stg3_valid_q <= out_stg2_valid_q;
    out_stg3_idx_q   <= out_stg2_idx_q;
end

reg signed [31:0] block_out[0:7];
reg signed [31:0] block_out_tmp;

always @ (posedge clk )
if (rst)
begin
    block_out[0] <= 32'b0;
    block_out[1] <= 32'b0;
    block_out[2] <= 32'b0;
    block_out[3] <= 32'b0;
    block_out[4] <= 32'b0;
    block_out[5] <= 32'b0;
    block_out[6] <= 32'b0;
    block_out[7] <= 32'b0;
    block_out_tmp <= 32'b0;
end
else if (out_stg3_valid_q)
begin
    if (out_stg3_idx_q == 3'd3)
    begin
        block_out[0] <= ((o_t0 + o_t7) >>> OUT_SHIFT);
        block_out_tmp <= ((o_t0 - o_t7) >>> OUT_SHIFT); // block_out[7]
        block_out[3] <= ((o_t3 + o_t4) >>> OUT_SHIFT);
        block_out[4] <= ((o_t3 - o_t4) >>> OUT_SHIFT);
    end

    if (out_stg3_idx_q == 3'd6)
        block_out[7] <= block_out_tmp;

    if (out_stg3_idx_q == 3'd7)
    begin
        block_out[2] <= ((o_t2 + o_s5) >>> OUT_SHIFT);
        block_out[5] <= ((o_t2 - o_s5) >>> OUT_SHIFT);
        block_out[1] <= ((o_t1 + o_s6) >>> OUT_SHIFT);
        block_out[6] <= ((o_t1 - o_s6) >>> OUT_SHIFT);
    end
end

reg [7:0] valid_q;

always @ (posedge clk )
if (rst)
    valid_q  <= 8'b0;
else if (img_start_i)
    valid_q  <= 8'b0;
else
    valid_q <= {valid_q[6:0], out_stg3_valid_q};

reg [5:0] ptr_q;

always @ (posedge clk )
if (rst)
    ptr_q <= 6'd0;
else if (img_start_i)
    ptr_q <= 6'd0;
else if (outport_valid_o)
    ptr_q <= ptr_q + 6'd1;

assign outport_valid_o = valid_q[6];
assign outport_data_o  = block_out[ptr_q[2:0]];

assign outport_idx_o   = ptr_q;



endmodule

module jpeg_idct_transpose
(
    // Inputs
     input           clk
    ,input           rst
    ,input           img_start_i
    ,input           img_end_i
    ,input           inport_valid_i
    ,input  [ 31:0]  inport_data_i
    ,input  [  5:0]  inport_idx_i
    ,input           outport_ready_i

    // Outputs
    ,output          inport_accept_o
    ,output          outport_valid_o
    ,output [ 31:0]  outport_data0_o
    ,output [ 31:0]  outport_data1_o
    ,output [ 31:0]  outport_data2_o
    ,output [ 31:0]  outport_data3_o
    ,output [  2:0]  outport_idx_o
);



reg         block_wr_q;
reg         block_rd_q;
reg [5:0]   rd_idx_q;
reg [3:0]   rd_addr_q;

wire [4:0]  wr_ptr_w = {block_wr_q, inport_idx_i[3:0]};

wire [31:0] outport_data0_w;
wire [31:0] outport_data1_w;
wire [31:0] outport_data2_w;
wire [31:0] outport_data3_w;

wire wr0_w = inport_valid_i && inport_accept_o && (inport_idx_i[5:4] == 2'd0);
wire wr1_w = inport_valid_i && inport_accept_o && (inport_idx_i[5:4] == 2'd1);
wire wr2_w = inport_valid_i && inport_accept_o && (inport_idx_i[5:4] == 2'd2);
wire wr3_w = inport_valid_i && inport_accept_o && (inport_idx_i[5:4] == 2'd3);

jpeg_idct_transpose_ram
u_ram0
(
     .clk0_i(clk)
    ,.rst0_i(rst)
    ,.clk1_i(clk)
    ,.rst1_i(rst)

    ,.addr0_i(wr_ptr_w)
    ,.data0_i(inport_data_i)
    ,.wr0_i(wr0_w)
    ,.data0_o()

    ,.addr1_i({block_rd_q, rd_addr_q})
    ,.data1_i(32'b0)
    ,.wr1_i(1'b0)
    ,.data1_o(outport_data0_w)
);

jpeg_idct_transpose_ram
u_ram1
(
     .clk0_i(clk)
    ,.rst0_i(rst)
    ,.clk1_i(clk)
    ,.rst1_i(rst)

    ,.addr0_i(wr_ptr_w)
    ,.data0_i(inport_data_i)
    ,.wr0_i(wr1_w)
    ,.data0_o()

    ,.addr1_i({block_rd_q, rd_addr_q})
    ,.data1_i(32'b0)
    ,.wr1_i(1'b0)
    ,.data1_o(outport_data1_w)
);

jpeg_idct_transpose_ram
u_ram2
(
     .clk0_i(clk)
    ,.rst0_i(rst)
    ,.clk1_i(clk)
    ,.rst1_i(rst)

    ,.addr0_i(wr_ptr_w)
    ,.data0_i(inport_data_i)
    ,.wr0_i(wr2_w)
    ,.data0_o()

    ,.addr1_i({block_rd_q, rd_addr_q})
    ,.data1_i(32'b0)
    ,.wr1_i(1'b0)
    ,.data1_o(outport_data2_w)
);

jpeg_idct_transpose_ram
u_ram3
(
     .clk0_i(clk)
    ,.rst0_i(rst)
    ,.clk1_i(clk)
    ,.rst1_i(rst)

    ,.addr0_i(wr_ptr_w)
    ,.data0_i(inport_data_i)
    ,.wr0_i(wr3_w)
    ,.data0_o()

    ,.addr1_i({block_rd_q, rd_addr_q})
    ,.data1_i(32'b0)
    ,.wr1_i(1'b0)
    ,.data1_o(outport_data3_w)
);

//-----------------------------------------------------------------
// Input Buffer
//-----------------------------------------------------------------
reg [1:0] block_ready_q;

always @ (posedge clk )
if (rst)
begin
    block_ready_q     <= 2'b0;
    block_wr_q        <= 1'b0;
    block_rd_q        <= 1'b0;
end
else if (img_start_i)
begin
    block_ready_q     <= 2'b0;
    block_wr_q        <= 1'b0;
    block_rd_q        <= 1'b0;
end
else
begin
    if (inport_valid_i && inport_idx_i == 6'd63 && inport_accept_o)
    begin
        block_ready_q[block_wr_q] <= 1'b1;
        block_wr_q                <= ~block_wr_q;
    end

    if (outport_valid_o && rd_idx_q[5:0] == 6'd63)
    begin
        block_ready_q[block_rd_q] <= 1'b0;
        block_rd_q <= ~block_rd_q;
    end
end

assign inport_accept_o = ~block_ready_q[block_wr_q];

//-----------------------------------------------------------------
// FSM
//-----------------------------------------------------------------
localparam STATE_W           = 2;
localparam STATE_IDLE        = 2'd0;
localparam STATE_SETUP       = 2'd1;
localparam STATE_ACTIVE      = 2'd2;

reg [STATE_W-1:0] state_q;
reg [STATE_W-1:0] next_state_r;

always @ *
begin
    next_state_r = state_q;

    case (state_q)
    STATE_IDLE:
    begin
        if (block_ready_q[block_rd_q] && outport_ready_i)
            next_state_r = STATE_SETUP;
    end
    STATE_SETUP:
    begin
        next_state_r = STATE_ACTIVE;
    end
    STATE_ACTIVE:
    begin
        if (outport_valid_o && rd_idx_q == 6'd63)
            next_state_r = STATE_IDLE;
    end
    default: ;
    endcase

    if (img_start_i)
        next_state_r = STATE_IDLE;
end

always @ (posedge clk )
if (rst)
    state_q <= STATE_IDLE;

... (showing lines 147-746 of 1381; use offset/limit for more)

[ASSISTANT]
  -> read_file(filename='starting_point.v', limit=640, offset=746)

[TOOL RESULT]
[Step 4/60]
    state_q <= STATE_IDLE;
else
    state_q <= next_state_r;

always @ (posedge clk )
if (rst)
    rd_idx_q <= 6'b0;
else if (img_start_i)
    rd_idx_q <= 6'b0;
else if (state_q == STATE_ACTIVE)
    rd_idx_q <= rd_idx_q + 6'd1;

always @ (posedge clk )
if (rst)
    rd_addr_q <= 4'b0;
else if (state_q == STATE_IDLE)
    rd_addr_q <= 4'b0;
else if (state_q == STATE_SETUP)
    rd_addr_q <= 4'd8;
else if (state_q == STATE_ACTIVE)
begin
    case (rd_idx_q[2:0])
    3'd0: rd_addr_q <= rd_addr_q - 4'd8;
    3'd1: rd_addr_q <= rd_addr_q + 4'd8;
    3'd2: ;
    3'd3: rd_addr_q <= rd_addr_q - 4'd8;
    3'd4: rd_addr_q <= rd_addr_q + 4'd8;
    3'd5: rd_addr_q <= rd_addr_q - 4'd8;
    3'd6: rd_addr_q <= rd_addr_q + 4'd1;
    3'd7: rd_addr_q <= rd_addr_q + 4'd8;
    endcase
end

assign outport_valid_o = (state_q == STATE_ACTIVE);
assign outport_idx_o   = rd_idx_q[2:0];
assign outport_data0_o = outport_data0_w;
assign outport_data1_o = outport_data1_w;
assign outport_data2_o = outport_data2_w;
assign outport_data3_o = outport_data3_w;


endmodule

module jpeg_idct_transpose_ram
(
    // Inputs
     input           clk0_i
    ,input           rst0_i
    ,input  [  4:0]  addr0_i
    ,input  [ 31:0]  data0_i
    ,input           wr0_i
    ,input           clk1_i
    ,input           rst1_i
    ,input  [  4:0]  addr1_i
    ,input  [ 31:0]  data1_i
    ,input           wr1_i

    // Outputs
    ,output [ 31:0]  data0_o
    ,output [ 31:0]  data1_o
);



//-----------------------------------------------------------------
// Dual Port RAM
// Mode: Read First
//-----------------------------------------------------------------
/* verilator lint_off MULTIDRIVEN */
reg [31:0]   ram [31:0] /*verilator public*/;
/* verilator lint_on MULTIDRIVEN */

reg [31:0] ram_read0_q;
reg [31:0] ram_read1_q;


// Synchronous write
always @ (posedge clk0_i)
begin
    if (wr0_i)
        ram[addr0_i][31:0] <= data0_i[31:0];

    ram_read0_q <= ram[addr0_i];
end

always @ (posedge clk1_i)
begin
    if (wr1_i)
        ram[addr1_i][31:0] <= data1_i[31:0];

    ram_read1_q <= ram[addr1_i];
end


assign data0_o = ram_read0_q;
assign data1_o = ram_read1_q;



endmodule

//                             V0.1

module jpeg_idct_y
//-----------------------------------------------------------------
// Params
//-----------------------------------------------------------------
#(
     parameter OUT_SHIFT        = 15
    ,parameter INPUT_WIDTH      = 32
)
//-----------------------------------------------------------------
// Ports
//-----------------------------------------------------------------
(
    // Inputs
     input           clk
    ,input           rst
    ,input           img_start_i
    ,input           img_end_i
    ,input           inport_valid_i
    ,input  [ 31:0]  inport_data0_i
    ,input  [ 31:0]  inport_data1_i
    ,input  [ 31:0]  inport_data2_i
    ,input  [ 31:0]  inport_data3_i
    ,input  [  2:0]  inport_idx_i

    // Outputs
    ,output          outport_valid_o
    ,output [ 31:0]  outport_data_o
    ,output [  5:0]  outport_idx_o
);




localparam [15:0] C1_16 = 4017; // cos( pi/16) x4096

    o_t5_6 <= 32'b0;

    case (out_stg2_idx_q)
    3'd0:
    begin
        o_t3 <= mul0 + mul1; // s3
    end
    3'd1:
    begin
        o_s7 <= mul0 + mul1;
        o_s6 <= mul2 + mul3;
        o_t0 <= mul4;        // s0
    end
    3'd2:
    begin
        o_t0 <= o_t0 + o_t3; // t0
        o_t3 <= o_t0 - o_t3; // t3
        o_t7 <= o_s6 + o_s7;
    end
    3'd3:
    begin
        o_t4 <= (mul0 - mul1) + (mul2 - mul3);
    end
    3'd4:
    begin
        o_t0 <= mul0 - mul1; // s4
        o_s5 <= mul2 - mul3;    
    end
    3'd5:
    begin
        o_t3 <= mul0 - mul1; // s2
        o_t4 <= mul4; // s1
        o_t5 <= o_t0 - o_s5;
        o_t6 <= o_s7 - o_s6;
    end
    3'd6:
    begin
        o_t1 <= o_t4 + o_t3;
        o_t2 <= o_t4 - o_t3;
        o_t6_5 <= o_t6 - o_t5;
        o_t5_6 <= o_t5 + o_t6;
    end
    default:
    begin
        o_s5 <= (o_t6_5 * 181) / 256; // 1/sqrt(2)
        o_s6 <= (o_t5_6 * 181) / 256; // 1/sqrt(2)
    end
    endcase

// Self-checking testbench for jpeg_idct_2d — the full 8x8 2-D IDCT
// (x-pass -> transpose -> y-pass) of ultraembedded/core_jpeg.
// Reads vectors.dat: one line per 8x8 block = 64 input coefficients (16-bit
// signed, 4 hex digits, row-major c[row][col]) followed by 64 expected outputs
// (32-bit, 8 hex digits, in ROW-MAJOR pixel order — outputs are matched via
// outport_idx_o, which labels each streamed value with its row-major position).
//
// Drive protocol: identical to the jpeg_idct_x benchmark (the upstream block
// serializer's fixed schedule): per row 8 back-to-back valid beats idx=0..7,
// EVEN coefficient set (c0,c2,c4,c6) at idx in {0,2,5,7}, ODD set (c1,c3,c5,c7)
// at idx in {1,3,4,6}; 8 rows back-to-back per 64-beat block; >= 2 idle cycles
// between blocks (valid=0, idx=0, data=0).
//
// Output contract (fixed schedule): 64 values per block streamed on
// outport_valid_o; the k-th value of a block carries
// outport_idx_o == {k[2:0], k[5:3]} (the y-pass un-transpose permutation) and
// must equal the expected output at that row-major position. Comparison is
// EXACT (integer outputs).
module tb;
  int total_checks;
  int total_errors;

  logic        clk;
  logic        rst;
  logic        img_start;
  logic        inport_valid;
  logic [15:0] inport_data0, inport_data1, inport_data2, inport_data3;
  logic [2:0]  inport_idx;
  logic        outport_valid;
  logic [31:0] outport_data;
  logic [5:0]  outport_idx;

  jpeg_idct_2d dut (
    .clk(clk), .rst(rst),
    .img_start_i(img_start), .img_end_i(1'b0),
    .inport_valid_i(inport_valid),
    .inport_data0_i(inport_data0), .inport_data1_i(inport_data1),
    .inport_data2_i(inport_data2), .inport_data3_i(inport_data3),
    .inport_idx_i(inport_idx),
    .outport_valid_o(outport_valid),
    .outport_data_o(outport_data),
    .outport_idx_o(outport_idx)
  );

  initial clk = 1'b0;
  always #5 clk = ~clk;

  // ---- load vectors.dat: flat token stream, 128 tokens per block ----------
  localparam MAX_BLOCKS = 1024;
  logic [15:0] coeffs [0:MAX_BLOCKS*64-1];
  logic [31:0] expy   [0:MAX_BLOCKS*64-1];
  int num_blocks;

  task automatic load_vectors;
    int fd, rc, n;
    logic [31:0] tok;
    logic [31:0] flat [0:MAX_BLOCKS*128-1];
    fd = $fopen("vectors.dat", "r");
    if (fd == 0) begin
      $display("ERROR: cannot open vectors.dat");
      $fatal(1);
    end
    n = 0;
    while (!$feof(fd)) begin
      rc = $fscanf(fd, "%h", tok);
      if (rc == 1) begin
        flat[n] = tok;
        n = n + 1;
      end
    end
    $fclose(fd);
    num_blocks = n / 128;
    if (num_blocks * 128 != n)
      $display("WARNING: %0d trailing tokens ignored", n - num_blocks * 128);
    for (int b = 0; b < num_blocks; b++)
      for (int i = 0; i < 64; i++) begin
        coeffs[b*64+i] = flat[b*128+i][15:0];
        expy[b*64+i]   = flat[b*128+64+i];
      end
  endtask

  // ---- output checker -------------------------------------------------------
  int out_cnt;
  bit checking;
  logic [5:0] k;

  always @(posedge clk) begin
    #1;
    if (checking && outport_valid) begin
      total_checks = total_checks + 1;
      k = out_cnt % 64;
      if (outport_idx !== {k[2:0], k[5:3]}) begin
        $display("TB_ERROR block=%0d out=%0d idx=%0d expected_idx=%0d",
                 out_cnt / 64, out_cnt % 64, outport_idx, {k[2:0], k[5:3]});
        total_errors = total_errors + 1;
      end
      if (outport_data !== expy[(out_cnt / 64) * 64 + outport_idx]) begin
        $display("TB_ERROR block=%0d pos=%0d expected_y=%08x actual_y=%08x",
                 out_cnt / 64, outport_idx, expy[(out_cnt / 64) * 64 + outport_idx],
                 outport_data);
        total_errors = total_errors + 1;
      end
`ifdef DUMP_Y
      $display("Y %02x %08x", outport_idx, outport_data);
`endif
      out_cnt = out_cnt + 1;
    end
  end

  // ---- driver ----------------------------------------------------------------
  task automatic drive_beat(input logic valid, input logic [2:0] kk,
                            input logic [15:0] d0, d1, d2, d3);
    @(negedge clk);
    inport_valid = valid;
    inport_idx   = kk;
    inport_data0 = d0;
    inport_data1 = d1;
    inport_data2 = d2;
    inport_data3 = d3;
  endtask

  initial begin
    total_checks = 0;
    total_errors = 0;
    out_cnt = 0;
    checking = 0;
    rst = 1;
    img_start = 0;
    inport_valid = 0;
    inport_idx = 0;
    inport_data0 = 0; inport_data1 = 0; inport_data2 = 0; inport_data3 = 0;

    load_vectors();

    repeat (4) @(negedge clk);
    rst = 0;
    @(negedge clk);
    img_start = 1;             // one-cycle image-start pulse
    @(negedge clk);
    img_start = 0;
    checking = 1;

    for (int b = 0; b < num_blocks; b++) begin
      for (int r = 0; r < 8; r++) begin
        for (int kb = 0; kb < 8; kb++) begin
          if (kb == 0 || kb == 2 || kb == 5 || kb == 7)
            drive_beat(1'b1, kb[2:0], coeffs[b*64+r*8+0], coeffs[b*64+r*8+2],

            drive_beat(1'b1, kb[2:0], coeffs[b*64+r*8+0], coeffs[b*64+r*8+2],
                                      coeffs[b*64+r*8+4], coeffs[b*64+r*8+6]);

    end

    if (total_errors != 0) $fatal(1, "FAIL");

[ASSISTANT]
Now I understand the design thoroughly. Let me lay out my plan:

**Plan:**
1. Rewrite the design from scratch with these optimizations while preserving bit-exact behavior:
   - **Constant-coefficient multipliers**: register only the 18/23-bit `a` operand + 13-bit constant `b` (instead of 32×32 multipliers)
   - **Merge pipeline stages**: drop the redundant second product register stage
   - **Narrow the transpose storage** to 21 bits (proven: x-pass outputs are `(t>>>11)` with |t| < 2³⁰, so outputs fit ±2¹⁹)
   - **Restructure the transpose**: replace 4 RAMs of 32 entries with 32-bit words (huge 32:1 mux decode) by 8 banks of 16×21-bit entries organized as {buffer, column} — read becomes a 16:1 mux selected by a simple column counter, write address is just the write-beat counter
   - **Replace `/256` division** with add-255-if-negative + arithmetic shift (truncation toward zero)
   - **Replace the 64-entry `ptr_conv` case** with direct `{ptr[2:0], ptr[5:3]}`
   - Drop dead read port / unnecessary resets on storage arrays
2. Evaluate, then iterate.

Let me write the design:
  -> create_file(filename='design_v1.v', content="//-----------------------------------------------------------------\n// jpeg_idct_2d — full 8x8 2-D IDCT (baseline JPEG decoder style).\n// Optimized rewrite of the core_jpeg composition:\n//   x-pass (AAN-style factorized 1-D IDCT, OUT_SHIFT=11)\n//   -> double-buffered corner turn (register files addressed by\n//      {buffer, column}; [ASSISTANT]
I found the bugs by careful analysis. Three issues in my v1:
1. `block_out[7]` was written at beat idx 3, but my 1-cycle-shorter pipeline means the next column's write overwrites it before it's streamed (the original uses a `tmp` register written at idx3, transferred at idx6 — I must restore that).
2. The transpose read mux never selected the column bank (always read column 0/1's storage).
3. Verified `even_set` pattern and timing constraints — rest is sound.

Let me write the corrected version:
  -> create_file(content="//-----------------------------------------------------------------\n// jpeg_idct_2d — full 8x8 2-D IDCT (baseline JPEG decoder style).\n// Optimized rewrite of the core_jpeg composition:\n//   x-pass (AAN-style factorized 1-D IDCT, OUT_SHIFT=11)\n//   -> double-buffered corner turn (8 register-file banks addressed\n//      by {buffer, row}; read fetches the even/odd row set per beat)\n//   -> y-pass (same 1-D pass, OUT_SHIFT=15) -> output streamer.\n// Bit-exact w.r.t. the original fixed-point behavior:\n//  - all arithmetic in 32-bit signed wrapping context\n//  - 1/sqrt(2) scaling (x*181)/256 with truncation toward zero\n//  - pass outputs arithmetic-shifted right (11 / 15)\n//-----------------------------------------------------------------\n\nmodule idct_1d #(\n    parameter integer IN_W      = 16,\n    parameter integer A_W       = 18,   // operand-a width (>= IN_W+2)\n    parameter integer OUT_SHIFT = 11,\n    parameter integer OUT_W     = 32,\n    parameter integer OUT_DELAY = 7,\n    parameter integer IDX_SWAP  = 0\n)(\n    input  wire                    clk,\n    input  wire                    rst,\n    input  wire                    img_start_i,\n    input  wire                    inport_valid_i,\n    input  wire [ 2:0]             inport_idx_i,\n    input  wire signed [IN_W-1:0]  inport_data0_i,\n    input  wire signed [IN_W-1:0]  inport_data1_i,\n    input  wire signed [IN_W-1:0]  inport_data2_i,\n    input  wire signed [IN_W-1:0]  inport_data3_i,\n    output wire                    outport_valid_o,\n    output wire signed [OUT_W-1:0] outport_data_o,\n    output wire [ 5:0]             outport_idx_o\n);\n\n    localparam signed [12:0] C1 = 13'sd4017; // cos( pi/16) x4096\n    localparam signed [12:0] C2 = 13'sd3784; // cos(2pi/16) x4096\n    localparam signed [12:0] C3 = 13'sd3406; // cos(3pi/16) x4096\n    localparam signed [12:0] C4 = 13'sd2896; // cos(4pi/16) x4096\n    localparam signed [12:0] C5 = 13'sd2276; // cos(5pi/16) x4096\n    localparam signed [12:0] C6 = 13'sd1567; // cos(6pi/16) x4096\n    localparam signed [12:0] C7 = 13'sd799;  // cos(7pi/16) x4096\n\n    // sign-extended input lanes\n    wire signed [A_W-1:0] din0 = inport_data0_i;\n    wire signed [A_W-1:0] din1 = inport_data1_i;\n    wire signed [A_W-1:0] din2 = inport_data2_i;\n    wire signed [A_W-1:0] din3 = inport_data3_i;\n\n    // operand registers (time-shared over the 8-beat schedule)\n    reg signed [A_W-1:0] ma0, ma1, ma2, ma3, ma4;\n    reg signed [12:0]    mb0, mb1, mb2, mb3, mb4;\n    reg signed [A_W-1:0] i0;\n\n    always @(posedge clk) begin\n        if (rst) begin\n            i0  <= {A_W{1'b0}};\n            ma0 <= {A_W{1'b0}}; ma1 <= {A_W{1'b0}};\n            ma2 <= {A_W{1'b0}}; ma3 <= {A_W{1'b0}};\n            ma4 <= {A_W{1'b0}};\n            mb0 <= 13'd0; mb1 <= 13'd0; mb2 <= 13'd0;\n            mb3 <= 13'd0; mb4 <= 13'd0;\n        end else begin\n            case (inport_idx_i)\n                3'd0: begin\n                    i0   <= din0 + din2;\n                    ma0  <= din1; mb0 <= C2;\n                    ma1  <= din3; mb1 <= C6;\n                end\n                3'd1: begin\n                    ma0  <= din0; mb0 <= C1;\n                    ma1  <= din3; mb1 <= C7;\n                    ma2  <= din2; mb2 <= C5;\n                    ma3  <= din1; mb3 <= C3;\n                    ma4  <= i0;   mb4 <= C4;\n                end\n                3'd2: begin\n                    i0   <= din0 - din2;\n                end\n                3'd3: begin\n                    ma0  <= din0; mb0 <= C7;\n                    ma1  <= din3; mb1 <= C1;\n                    ma2  <= din2; mb2 <= C3;\n                    ma3  <= din1; mb3 <= C5;\n                end\n                3'd4: begin\n                    ma0  <= din0; mb0 <= C7;\n                    ma1  <= din3; mb1 <= C1;\n                    ma2  <= din2; mb2 <= C3;\n                    ma3  <= din1; mb3 <= C5;\n                end\n                3'd5: begin\n                    ma0  <= din1; mb0 <= C6;\n                    ma1  <= din3; mb1 <= C2;\n                    ma4  <= i0;   mb4 <= C4;\n                end\n                default: ;\n            endcase\n        end\n    end\n\n    // products (32-bit signed wrapping context, as the original)\n    reg signed [31:0] mq0, mq1, mq2, mq3, mq4;\n\n    always @(posedge clk) begin\n        if (rst) begin\n            mq0 <= 32'd0; mq1 <= 32'd0; mq2 <= 32'd0;\n            mq3 <= 32'd0; mq4 <= 32'd0;\n        end else begin\n            mq0 <= ma0 * mb0;\n            mq1 <= ma1 * mb1;\n            mq2 <= ma2 * mb2;\n            mq3 <= ma3 * mb3;\n            mq4 <= ma4 * mb4;\n        end\n    end\n\n    // valid/idx pipeline (2 stages to the accumulate stage)\n    reg       stg0_v, stg1_v, stg2_v;\n    reg [2:0] stg0_i, stg1_i, stg2_i;\n\n    always @(posedge clk) begin\n        if (rst) begin\n            stg0_v <= 1'b0; // s4\n                    o_s5 <= mq2 - mq3;\n                end\n                3'd5: begin\n                    o_t3 <= mq0 - mq1; // s2\n                    o_t4 <= mq4;       // s1\n                    o_t5 <= o_t0 - o_s5;\n                    o_t6 <= o_s7 - o_s6;\n                end\n                3'd6: begin\n                    o_t1  <= o_t4 + o_t3;\n                    o_t2  <= o_t4 - o_t3;\n                    o_t65 <= o_t6 - o_t5;\n                    o_t56 <= o_t5 + o_t6;\n                end\n                default: begin\n                    o_s5 <= d5q; // 1/sqrt(2)\n                    o_s6 <= d6q; // 1/sqrt(2)\n                end\n            endcase\n        end\n    end\n\n    // output stage\n    reg signed [OUT_W-1:0] block_out [0:7];\n    reg signed [OUT_W-1:0] bo_tmp;\n\n    always @(posedge clk) begin\n        if (rst) begin\n            block_out[0] <= {OUT_W{1'b0}};\n            block_out[1] <= {OUT_W{1'b0}};\n            block_out[2] <= {OUT_W{1'b0}};\n            block_out[3] <= {OUT_W{1'b0}};\n            block_out[4] <= {OUT_W{1'b0}};\n            block_out[5] <= {OUT_W{1'b0}};\n            block_out[6] <= {OUT_W{1'b0}};\n            block_out[7] <= {OUT_W{1'b0}};\n            bo_tmp       <= {OUT_W{1'b0}};\n        end else if (stg2_v) begin\n            case (stg2_i)\n                3'd3: begin\n                    block_out[0] <= (o_t0 + o_t7) >>> OUT_SHIFT;\n                    bo_tmp       <= (o_t0 - o_t7) >>> OUT_SHIFT; // -> [7]\n                    block_out[3] <= (o_t3 + o_t4) >>> OUT_SHIFT;\n                    block_out[4] <= (o_t3 - o_t4) >>> OUT_SHIFT;\n                end\n                3'd6: begin\n                    block_out[7] <= bo_tmp;\n                end\n                3'd7: begin\n                    block_out[2] <= (o_t2 + o_s5) >>> OUT_SHIFT;\n                    block_out[5] <= (o_t2 - o_s5) >>> OUT_SHIFT;\n                    block_out[1] <= (o_t1 + o_s6) >>> OUT_SHIFT;\n                    block_out[6] <= (o_t1 - o_s6) >>> OUT_SHIFT;\n                end\n                default: ;\n            endcase\n        end\n    end\n\n    // output streamer\n    reg [OUT_DELAY-1:0] vpipe;\n    reg [5:0]           ptr;\n\n    always @(posedge clk) begin\n        if (rst || img_start_i) begin\n            vpipe <= {(OUT_DELAY){1'b0}};\n            ptr   <= 6'd0;\n        end else begin\n            vpipe <= {vpipe[OUT_DELAY-2:0], stg2_v};\n            if (vpipe[OUT_DELAY-1])\n                ptr <= ptr + 6'd1;\n        end\n    end\n\n    assign outport_valid_o = vpipe[OUT_DELAY-1];\n    assign outport_data_o  = block_out[ptr[2:0]];\n    assign outport_idx_o   = IDX_SWAP ? {ptr[2:0], ptr[5:3]} : ptr;\n\nendmodule\n\n//-----------------------------------------------------------------\n// Double-buffered corner turn.\n// Storage: 8 banks. Bank p holds columns 2p (memA) and 2p+1 (memB);\n// each bank entry is indexed by {buffer, row}. A write beat (arriving\n// in row-major order) uses the write-beat counter as address; a read\n// beat for (column c, row r) fetches the 4-lane even row set\n// (rows 0,2,4,6) or odd row set (rows 1,3,5,7) of column c.\n//-----------------------------------------------------------------\nmodule idct_transpose #(\n    parameter integer DW = 21\n)(\n    input  wire                 clk,\n    input  wire                 rst,\n    input  wire                 img_start_i,\n    input  wire                 in_valid,\n    input  wire signed [DW-1:0] in_data,\n    output wire wire [ 2:0]          out_idx\n);\n\n    reg [5:0] wptr;\n    reg [5:0] rptr;\n    reg       reading;\n    reg       wr_sel, rd_sel;\n    reg       ready0, ready1;\n\n    // write side (gated if the target buffer is still unread)\n    wire wr_en   = in_valid && !(wr_sel ? ready1 : ready0);\n    wire wr_last = wr_en && (wptr == 6'd63);\n\n    reg signed [DW-1:0] memA [0:3][0:15]; // even columns (c[0]=0)\n    reg signed [DW-1:0] memB [0:3][0:15]; // odd  columns (c[0]=1)\n\n    // write: column = wptr[5:3], row = wptr[2:0], buffer = wr_sel\n    wire        w_odd = wptr[3];\n    wire [1:0] memA[w_p][w_e] <= in_data;\n        end\n    end\n\n    // read: column = rptr[5:3], row-set selector = rptr[2:0]\n    wire [1:0] p  = rptr[4:3];\n    wire       rb = rptr[3]; // odd column select\n     reg signed [DW-1:0] memA [0:3][0:15]; // even columns (c[0]=0)
    reg signed [DW-1:0] memB [0:3][0:15]; // odd  columns (c[0]=1)

    // write: column = wptr[5:3], row = wptr[2:0], buffer = wr_sel

    wire [1:0]  w_p   = wptr[4:3];
    wire [3:0]  w_e   = {wr_sel, wptr[2:0]};

        if (wr_en) begin
            if (w_odd) memB[w_p][w_e] <= in_data;
            else       memA[w_p][w_e] <= in_data;
        end

**FOUND THE BUG!** I mixed up the field layout of the x-pass output index: `x_idx = ptr` is the **row-major** index `k = {row[5:3], col[2:0]}` — so during writes, bits [5:3] are the ROW and [2:0] are the COLUMN (I had them swapped).   -> create_file('{"content":"//-----------------------------------------------------------------\\n// jpeg_idct_2d — full 8x8 2-D IDCT (baseline JPEG decoder style).\\n// Row pass (AAN-style factorized 1-D IDCT, OUT_SHIFT=11, constant-\\n// coefficient time-shared multipliers) fused with a double-buffered\\n// corner turn (16 register-file banks addressed by {column-pair,\\n// column-parity, buffer}, 8 rows each), then the column pass\\n// (same 1-D pass, OUT_SHIFT=15) and the output streamer.\\n// Bit-exact fixed-point behavior: 32-bit signed wrapping arithmetic,\\n// (x*181)/256 truncating toward zero, arithmetic right shifts.\\n//-----------------------------------------------------------------\\n\\n//-----------------------------------------------------------------\\n// Row pass + corner-turn tile.\\n// The 1-D pass accumulates 8 outputs per row over the 8-beat input\\n// schedule; at beats idx==3 and idx==7 four fresh outputs are\\n// available and are written straight into the corner-turn storage\\n// (one value per bank). [ASSISTANT]
That top-level streamer attempt was wrong — the y-pass needs the original's `block_out` registers (beat 6 streams a value computed at beat 3, so it must be held). The clean design: keep v2's proven `idct_1d` and only rebuild the transpose as 4 lane-banks. Let me rewrite v3 properly:

[ASSISTANT]
Now I see the remaining bug clearly: my v3 write only covered banks 0–1 (`default: ;` dropped columns 4–7). Let me rewrite the transpose with a **lane-organized** layout, which also has the smallest read mux (each lane reads its own array at address {buffer, col}, with only a 2:1 sub-bank mux — no bank mux at all):
  -> edit_file(filename='design_v3.v', new_string="//-----------------------------------------------------------------\n// Double-buffered corner turn (lane-organized storage).\n// Each output lane j owns two 16-entry arrays (sub-banks A/B) indexed\n// by {buffer, col}; sub-bank A holds row 2j values, B holds row 2j+1.\n// Write beat (row pass, row-major k_x = {row, col}):\n//   array = row[2:1], sub-bank = row[0], entry = {buffer, col}.\n// Read beat (column pass, k_y = {row, col}):\n//   lane j reads its arrays at entry {buffer, col}; the even-set beats\n//   (k_y in {0,2,5,7}) take sub-bank A (rows 0,2,4,6), the odd-set\n//   beats (k_y in {1,3,4,6}) take sub-bank B (rows 1,3,5,7).\n// Read decode is a 2:1 mux per lane (no bank mux).\n//-----------------------------------------------------------------\nmodule idct_transpose #(\n    parameter integer DW = 21\n)(\n    input  wire                 clk,\n    input  wire                 rst,\n    input  wire                 img_start_i,\n    input  wire                 in_valid,\n    input  wire signed [DW-1:0] in_data,\n    output wire                 out_valid,\n    output wire signed [DW-1:0] out_data0,\n    output wire signed [DW-1:0] out_data1,\n    output wire signed [DW-1:0] out_data2,\n    output wire signed [DW-1:0] out_data3,\n    output wire [ 2:0]          out_idx\n);\n\n    reg [5:0] wptr;\n    reg [5:0] rptr;\n    reg       reading;\n    reg       wr_sel, rd_sel;\n    reg       ready0, ready1;\n\n    // write side (gated if the target buffer is still unread)\n    wire wr_en   = in_valid && !(wr_sel ? ready1 : ready0);\n    wire wr_last = wr_en && (wptr == 6'd63);\n\n    reg signed [DW-1:0] l0a [0:15]; // lane 0, rows 0\n    reg signed [DW-1:0] l0b [0:15]; // lane 0, rows 1\n    reg signed [DW-1:0] l1a [0:15]; // lane 1, rows 2\n    reg signed [DW-1:0] l1b [0:15]; // lane 1, rows 3\n    reg signed [DW-1:0] l2a [0:15]; // lane 2, rows 4\n    reg signed [DW-1:0] l2b [0:15]; // lane 2, rows 5\n    reg signed [DW-1:0] l3a [0:15]; // lane 3, rows 6\n    reg signed [DW-1:0] l3b [0:15]; // lane 3, rows 7\n\n    // write beat k_x = {row[5:3], col[2:0]}\n    wire [1:0] w_arr = wptr[5:4];           // row[2:1] -> lane pair\n    wire       w_sub = wptr[3];             // row[0]   -> A/B\n    wire [3:0] w_e    = {wr_sel, wptr[2:0]}; // {buffer, col}\n\n    always @(posedge clk) begin\n        if (wr_en) begin\n            case (w_arr)\n                2'd0: begin if (w_sub) l0b[w_e] <= in_data; else l0a[w_e] <= in_data; end\n                2'd1: begin if (w_sub) l1b[w_e] <= in_data; else l1a[w_e] <= in_data; end\n                2'd2: begin if (w_sub) l2b[w_e] <= in_data; else l2a[w_e] <= in_data; end\n                default: begin if (w_sub) l3b[w_e] <= in_data; else l3a[w_e] <= in_data; end\n            endcase\n        end\n    end\n\n    // read beat k_y = {row[5:3], col[2:0]}\n    wire [3:0] r_e = {rd_sel, rptr[2:0]};   // {buffer, col}\n    wire       ro  = rptr[2];               // even/odd row-set flag\n\n    wire signed [DW-1:0] v0 = ro ?     reg signed [DW-1:0] l0b [0:15]; // lane 0, rows 1
    reg signed [DW-1:0] l1a [0:15]; // lane 1, rows 2
    reg signed [DW-1:0] l1b [0:15]; // lane 1, rows 3
    reg signed [DW-1:0] l2a [0:15]; // lane 2, rows 4
    reg signed [DW-1:0] l2b [0:15]; // lane 2, rows 5
    reg signed [DW-1:0] l3a [0:15]; // lane 3, rows 6
    reg signed [DW-1:0] l3b [0:15]; // lane 3, rows 7

    // write beat k_x = {row[5:3], col[2:0]}
    wire [1:0] w_arr = wptr[5:4];           // row[2:1] -> lane pair
    wire       w_sub = wptr[3];             // row[0]   -> A/B
    wire [3:0] w_e    = {wr_sel, wptr[2:0]}; // {buffer, col}

    always @(posedge clk) begin
        if (wr_en) begin
            case (w_arr)
                2'd0: begin if (w_sub) l0b[w_e] <= in_data; else l0a[w_e] <= in_data; end
                2'd1: begin if (w_sub) l1b[w_e] <= in_data; else l1a[w_e] <= in_data; end
                2'd2: begin if (w_sub) l2b[w_e] <= in_data; else l2a[w_e] <= in_data; end
                default: begin if (w_sub) l3b[w_e] <= in_data; else l3a[w_e] <= in_data; end
            endcase
        end
    end

    // read beat k_y = {col[5:3], kb[2:0]}: column c = rptr[5:3] is being
    // fed to the y-pass; each lane fetches its entry for column c
    wire [3:0] r_e = {rd_sel, rptr[5:3]};   // {buffer, col}
    // even/odd row-set flag of the beat: the y-pass consumes the even row
    // set (rows 0,2,4,6) at kb in {0,2,5} and the odd set (1,3,5,7) at
    // kb in {1,3,4} (kb in {6,7} load no operands -> don't care)
    wire       ro  = rptr[0] ^ rptr[2];

    wire signed [DW-1:0] v0 = ro ? l0b[r_e] : l0a[r_e];
    wire signed [DW-1:0] v1 = ro ? l1b[r_e] : l1a[r_e];
    wire signed [DW-1:0] v2 = ro ? l2b[r_e] : l2a[r_e];
    wire signed [DW-1:0] v3 = ro ? l3b[r_e] : l3a[r_e];

    assign out_data0 = v0;
    assign out_data1 = v1;
    assign out_data2 = v2;
    assign out_data3 = v3;
    assign out_idx   = rptr[2:0];
    assign out_valid = reading;

    always @(posedge clk) begin
        if (rst || img_start_i) begin
            wptr    <= 6'd0;
            rptr    <= 6'd0;
            reading <= 1'b0;
            wr_sel  <= 1'b0;
            rd_sel  <= 1'b0;
            ready0  <= 1'b0;
            ready1  <= 1'b0;
        end else begin
            if (wr_last) begin
                wptr <= 6'd0;
                if (wr_sel) begin
                    ready1 <= 1'b1;
                    wr_sel <= 1'b0;
                end else begin
                    ready0 <= 1'b1;
                    wr_sel <= 1'b1;
                end
            end else if (wr_en) begin
                wptr <= wptr + 6'd1;

                end

                reading <= 1'b1;
                rptr    <= 6'd0;
            end
        end
    end

[ASSISTANT]
The debug evidence plus careful re-derivation of the original's pipeline timing revealed the root cause: **my passes write `block_out` one cycle earlier than the original** (my accumulate stage is 1 cycle shorter), which breaks the original's delicate same-cycle read-before-write alignment in the output streamer (elements 4 and 7 are read in the same cycle the next row/column overwrites them). The fix: reduce the streamer delay by 1 (OUT_DELAY 8→7), which restores the exact original alignment (stream delay 10 with writes at +3, instead of 11 with writes at +4):
  -> edit_file(filename='design_v3.v', new_string='    idct_1d #(\n        .IN_W      (16),\n        .A_W       (18),\n        .OUT_SHIFT (11),\n        .OUT_W     (21),\n        .OUT_DELAY (7),\n        .IDX_SWAP  (0)\n    ) u_idct_x (', old_string='    idct_1d #(\n        .IN_W      (16),\n        .A_W       (18),\n        .OUT_SHIFT (11),\n        .OUT_W     (21),\n        .OUT_DELAY (8),\n        .IDX_SWAP  (0)\n    ) u_idct_x (')

  -> edit_file(filename='design_v3.v', new_string='    wire signed [DW-1:0] v0 = ro ? l0b[r_e] : l0a[r_e];\n    wire signed [DW-1:0] v1 = ro ? l1b[r_e] : l1a[r_e];\n    wire signed [DW-1:0] v2 = ro ? l2b[r_e] : l2a[r_e];\n    wire signed [DW-1:0] v3 = ro ? l3b[r_e] : l3a[r_e];\n\n    // registered read side: keeps the 16:1 read mux off the y-pass\n    // arithmetic path (the whole y-pass schedule shifts uniformly)\n    reg signed [DW-1:0] q0, q1, q2, q3;\n    reg                 vq;\n    reg [2:0]           iq;\n\n    always @(posedge clk) begin\n        q0 <= v0;\n        q1 <= v1;\n        q2 <= v2;\n        q3 <= v3;\n        vq <= reading;\n        iq <= rptr[2:0];\n    end\n\n    assign out_data0 = q0;\n    assign out_data1 = q1;\n    assign out_data2 = q2;\n    assign out_data3 = q3;\n    assign out_idx   = iq;\n    assign out_valid = vq;', old_string='    wire signed [DW-1:0] v0 = ro ? l0b[r_e] : l0a[r_e];\n    wire signed [DW-1:0] v1 = ro ? l1b[r_e] : l1a[r_e];\n    wire signed [DW-1:0] v2 = ro ? l2b[r_e] : l2a[r_e];\n    wire signed [DW-1:0] v3 = ro ? l3b[r_e] : l3a[r_e];\n\n    assign out_data0 = v0;\n    assign out_data1 = v1;\n    assign out_data2 = v2;\n    assign out_data3 = v3;\n    assign out_idx   = rptr[2:0];\n    assign out_valid = reading;')

[ASSISTANT]
**v3 PASSES all 20672 checks** — the optimized design is functionally correct (area 4101 vs original 4274, but delay 4075ps is worse). The critical path is the transpose read: `rptr` drives 8 arrays' 16:1 mux decodes (huge fanout → 2541ps on the register pin alone).

Fix: **per-array registered read address** (each of the 8 storage arrays gets its own copy of the address register, so each register drives only its own array's mux), plus a per-array write-enable register so `wptr` only drives tiny decode logic. The address register replaces the data register in the pipeline (same depth), so the schedule is preserved exactly:
  -> create_file(content="//-----------------------------------------------------------------\n// jpeg_idct_2d — full 8x8 2-D IDCT (baseline JPEG decoder style).\n// Row pass (AAN factorized 1-D IDCT, OUT_SHIFT=11) -> double-buffered\n// corner turn -> column pass (OUT_SHIFT=15) -> output streamer.\n// Bit-exact w.r.t. the original fixed-point behavior:\n//  - all arithmetic in 32-bit signed wrapping context\n//  - 1/sqrt(2) scaling (x*181)/256 with truncation toward zero\n//  - pass outputs arithmetic-shifted right (11 / 15)\n//\n// Implementation notes vs. the original core_jpeg RTL:\n//  * constant-coefficient multipliers (one operand is a hardwired cos\n//    constant), single product register stage (the original has two)\n//  * (x*181)/256 implemented as add-255-if-negative then arithmetic\n//    shift (exact: signed division truncating toward zero)\n//  * corner turn: 8 storage arrays (4 output lanes x even/odd row\n//    sub-bank), 16 entries each, indexed {buffer, beat-within-row};\n//    each array owns a private registered read address (so no counter\n//    bit drives more than one array's read mux) and a private\n//    registered write enable (the write address/data are shared\n//    pipeline registers); the per-lane even/odd selection is a 2:1 mux\n//    on the registered read addresses' outputs\n//  * y-pass idx label generated as {k[2:0], k[5:3]} directly\n//-----------------------------------------------------------------\n\nmodule idct_1d #(\n    parameter integer IN_W      = 16,\n    parameter integer A_W       = 18,   // operand-a width (>= IN_W+2)\n    parameter integer OUT_SHIFT = 11,\n    parameter integer OUT_W     = 32,\n    parameter integer OUT_DELAY = 8,\n    parameter integer IDX_SWAP  = 0\n)(\n    input  wire                    clk,\n    input  wire                    rst,\n    input  wire                    img_start_i,\n    input  wire                    inport_valid_i,\n    input  wire [ 2:0]             inport_idx_i,\n    input  wire signed [IN_W-1:0]  inport_data0_i,\n    input  wire signed [IN_W-1:0]  inport_data1_i,\n    input  wire signed [IN_W-1:0]  inport_data2_i,\n    input  wire signed [IN_W-1:0]  inport_data3_i,\n    output wire                    outport_valid_o,\n    output wire signed [OUT_W-1:0] outport_data_o,\n    output wire [ 5:0]             outport_idx_o\n);\n\n    localparam signed [12:0] C1 = 13'sd4017; // cos( pi/16) x4096\n    localparam signed [12:0] C2 = 13'sd3784; // cos(2pi/16) x4096\n    localparam signed [12:0] C3 = 13'sd3406; // cos(3pi/16) x4096\n    localparam signed [12:0] C4 = 13'sd2896; // cos(4pi/16) x4096\n    localparam signed [12:0] C5 = 13'sd2276; // cos(5pi/16) x4096\n    localparam signed [12:0] C6 = 13'sd1567; // cos(6pi/16) x4096\n    localparam signed [12:0] C7 = 13'sd799;  // cos(7pi/16) x4096\n\n    // sign-extended input lanes\n    wire signed [A_W-1:0] din0 = inport_data0_i;\n    wire signed [A_W-1:0] din1 = inport_data1_i;\n    wire signed [A_W-1:0] din2 = inport_data2_i;\n    wire signed [A_W-1:0] din3 = inport_data3_i;\n\n    // operand registers (time-shared over the 8-beat schedule)\n    reg signed [A_W-1:0] ma0, ma1, ma2, ma3, ma4;\n    reg signed [12:0]    mb0, mb1, mb2, mb3, mb4;\n    reg signed [A_W-1:0] i0;\n\n    always @(posedge clk) begin\n        if (rst) begin\n            i0  <= {A_W{1'b0}};\n            ma0 <= {A_W{1'b0}}; ma1 <= {A_W{1'b0}};\n            ma2 <= {A_W{1'b0}}; ma3 <= {A_W{1'b0}};\n            ma4 <= {A_W{1'b0}};\n            mb0 <= 13'd0; mb1 <= 13'd0; mb2 <= 13'd0;\n            mb3 <= 13'd0; mb4 <= 13'd0;\n        end else begin\n            case (inport_idx_i)\n                3'd0: begin\n                    i0   <= din0 + din2;\n                    ma0  <= din1; mb0 <= C2;\n                    ma1  <= din3; mb1 <= C6;\n                end\n                3'd1: begin\n                    ma0  <= din0; mb0 <= C1;\n                    ma1  <= din3; mb1 <= C7;\n                    ma2  <= din2; mb2 <= C5;\n                    ma3  <= din1; mb3 <= C3;\n                    ma4  <= i0;   mb4 <= C4;\n                end\n                3'd2: begin\n                    i0   <= din0 - din2;\n                end\n                3'd3: begin\n                    ma0  <= din0; mb0 <= C7;\n                    ma1  <= din3; mb1 <= C1;\n                    ma2  <= din2; mb2 <= C3;\n                    ma3  <= din1; mb3 <= C5;\n                end\n                3'd4: begin\n                    ma0  <= din0; mb0 <= C7;\n                    ma1  <= din3; mb1 <= C1;\n                    ma2  <= din2; mb2 <= C3;\n                    ma3  <= din1; mb3 <= C5;\n                end\n                3'd5: begin\n                    ma0  <= din1; mb0 <= C6;\n                    ma1  <= din3; mb1 <= C2;\n                    ma4  <= i0;   mb4 <= C4;\n                end\n                default: ;\n            endcase\n        end\n    end\n\n    // products (32-bit signed wrapping context, as the original)\n    reg signed [31:0] mq0, mq1, mq2, mq3, mq4;\n\n    always @(posedge clk) begin\n        if (rst) begin\n            mq0 <= 32'd0; mq1 <= 32'd0; mq2 <= 32'd0;\n            mq3 <= 32'd0; mq4 <= 32'd0;\n        end else begin\n            mq0 <= ma0 * mb0;\n            mq1 <= ma1 * mb1;\n            mq2 <= ma2 * mb2;\n            mq3 <= ma3 * mb3;\n            mq4 <= ma4 * mb4;\n        end\n    end\n\n    // valid/idx pipeline (2 stages to the accumulate stage)\n    reg       stg0_v, stg1_v, stg2_v;\n    reg [2:0] stg0_i, stg1_i, stg2_i;\n\n    always @(posedge clk) begin\n        if (rst) begin\n            stg0_v <= 1'b0; stg1_v <= 1'b0; stg2_v <= 1'b0;\n            stg0_i <= 3'd0; stg1_i <= 3'd0; stg2_i <= 3'd0;\n        end else begin\n            stg0_v <= inport_valid_i; stg0_i <= inport_idx_i;\n            stg1_v <= stg0_v;         stg1_i <= stg0_i;\n            stg2_v <= stg1_v;         stg2_i <= stg1_i;\n        end\n    end\n\n    // accumulate stage\n    reg signed [31:0] o_s5, o_s6, o_s7;\n    reg signed [31:0] o_t0, o_t1, o_t2, o_t3, o_t4, o_t5, o_t6, o_t7;\n    reg signed [31:0] o_t65, o_t56;\n\n    // (x*181)/256 : product wraps at 32 bits, divide truncates toward zero\n    wire signed [31:0] d5p = o_t65 * 181;\n    wire signed [31:0] d6p = o_t56 * 181;\n    wire signed [31:0] d5q = (d5p[31] ? (d5p + 32'sd255) : d5p) >>> 8;\n    wire signed [31:0] d6q = (d6p[31] ? (d6p + 32'sd255) : d6p) >>> 8;\n\n    always @(posedge clk) begin\n        if (rst) begin\n            o_s5  <= 32'd0; o_s6  <= 32'd0; o_s7  <= 32'd0;\n            o_t0  <= 32'd0; o_t1  <= 32'd0; o_t2  <= 32'd0;\n            o_t3  <= 32'd0; o_t4  <= 32'd0; o_t5  <= 32'd0;\n            o_t6  <= 32'd0; o_t7  <= 32'd0;\n            o_t65 <= 32'd0; o_t56 <= 32'd0;\n        end else begin\n            case (stg1_i)\n                3'd0: begin\n                    o_t3 <= mq0 + mq1; // s3\n                end\n                3'd1: begin\n                    o_s7 <= mq0 + mq1;\n                    o_s6 <= mq2 + mq3;\n                    o_t0 <= mq4;       // s0\n                end\n                3'd2: begin\n                    o_t0 <= o_t0 + o_t3; // t0\n                    o_t3 <= o_t0 - o_t3; // t3\n                    o_t7 <= o_s6 + o_s7;\n                end\n                3'd3: begin\n                    o_t4 <= (mq0 - mq1) + (mq2 - mq3);\n                end\n                3'd4: begin\n                    o_t0 <= mq0 - mq1; // s4\n                    o_s5 <= mq2 - mq3;\n                end\n                3'd5: begin\n                    o_t3 <= mq0 - mq1; // s2\n                    o_t4 <= mq4;       // s1\n                    o_t5 <= o_t0 - o_s5;\n                    o_t6 <= o_s7 - o_s6;\n                end\n                3'd6: begin\n                    o_t1  <= o_t4 + o_t3;\n                    o_t2  <= o_t4 - o_t3;\n                    o_t65 <= o_t6 - o_t5;\n                    o_t56 <= o_t5 + o_t6;\n                end\n                default: begin\n                    o_s5 <= d5q; // 1/sqrt(2)\n                    o_s6 <= d6q; // 1/sqrt(2)\n                end\n            endcase\n        end\n    end\n\n    // output stage\n    reg signed [OUT_W-1:0] block_out [0:7];\n    reg signed [OUT_W-1:0] bo_tmp;\n\n    always @(posedge clk) begin\n        if (rst) begin\n            block_out[0] <= {OUT_W{1'b0}};\n            block_out[1] <= {OUT_W{1'b0}};\n            block_out[2] <= {OUT_W{1'b0}};\n            block_out[3] <= {OUT_W{1'b0}};\n            block_out[4] <= {OUT_W{1'b0}};\n            block_out[5] <= {OUT_W{1'b0}};\n            block_out[6] <= {OUT_W{1'b0}};\n            block_out[7] <= {OUT_W{1'b0}};\n            bo_tmp       <= {OUT_W{1'b0}};\n        end else if (stg2_v) begin\n            case (stg2_i)\n                3'd3: begin\n                    block_out[0] <= (o_t0 + o_t7) >>> OUT_SHIFT;\n                    bo_tmp       <= (o_t0 - o_t7) >>> OUT_SHIFT; // -> [7]\n                    block_out[3] <= (o_t3 + o_t4) >>> OUT_SHIFT;\n                    block_out[4] <= (o_t3 - o_t4) >>> OUT_SHIFT;\n                end\n                3'd6: begin\n                    block_out[7] <= bo_tmp;\n                end\n                3'd7: begin\n                    block_out[2] <= (o_t2 + o_s5) >>> OUT_SHIFT;\n                    block_out[5] <= (o_t2 - o_s5) >>> OUT_SHIFT;\n                    block_out[1] <= (o_t1 + o_s6) >>> OUT_SHIFT;\n                    block_out[6] <= (o_t1 - o_s6) >>> OUT_SHIFT;\n                end\n                default: ;\n            endcase\n        end\n    end\n\n    // output streamer\n    reg [OUT_DELAY-1:0] vpipe;\n    reg [5:0]           ptr;\n\n    always @(posedge clk) begin\n        if (rst || img_start_i) begin\n            vpipe <= {(OUT_DELAY){1'b0}};\n            ptr   <= 6'd0;\n        end else begin\n            vpipe <= {vpipe[OUT_DELAY-2:0], stg2_v};\n            if (vpipe[OUT_DELAY-1])\n                ptr <= ptr + 6'd1;\n        end\n    end\n\n    assign outport_valid_o = vpipe[OUT_DELAY-1];\n    assign outport_data_o  = block_out[ptr[2:0]];\n    assign outport_idx_o   = IDX_SWAP ? {ptr[2:0], ptr[5:3]} : ptr;\n\nendmodule\n\n//-----------------------------------------------------------------\n// Double-buffered corner turn.\n//\n// Storage: 8 arrays (lane j in 0..3 x sub-bank s in {0,1}), 16 entries\n// each, entry index {buffer, b} where b is the beat-within-row index\n// of the row pass (and, symmetrically, the beat-within-column index of\n// the column pass).  A write beat carrying the row-pass value for\n// (row, col) targets array {row[2:1], row[0]} entry {buffer, col};\n// all four lanes of a beat share the same (array-group, entry) decode.\n// A read beat for column c fetches, from every array, entry\n// {buffer, c}; lane j presents sub-bank 0 (even rows) or 1 (odd rows)\n// of its own array pair according to the beat's row-set flag.\n//\n// Timing structure: every array owns a private registered read address\n// and a private registered write enable; the write data/address live in\n// shared pipeline registers.  Hence no control counter drives more than\n// a handful of gates, and the read mux output goes straight into the\n// column-pass operand registers.\n//-----------------------------------------------------------------\nmodule idct_transpose #(\n    parameter integer DW = 21\n)(\n    input  wire                 clk,\n    input  wire                 rst,\n    input  wire                 img_start_i,\n    input  wire                 in_valid,\n    input  wire signed [DW-1:0] in_data,\n    output wire                 out_valid,\n    output wire signed [DW-1:0] out_data0,\n    output wire signed [DW-1:0] out_data1,\n    output wire signed [DW-1:0] out_data2,\n    output wire signed [DW-1:0] out_data3,\n    output wire [ 2:0]          out_idx\n);\n\n    reg [5:0] wptr;\n    reg [5:0] rptr;\n    reg       reading;\n    reg       wr_sel, rd_sel;\n    reg       ready0, ready1;\n\n    // write side (gated if the target buffer is still unread)\n    wire wr_en   = in_valid && !(wr_sel ? ready1 : ready0);\n    wire wr_last = wr_en && (wptr == 6'd63);\n\n    // shared write pipeline registers\n    reg signed [DW-1:0] wd_q;\n    reg [3:0]           wa_q;   // {buffer, col}\n\n    always @(posedge clk) begin\n        wd_q <= in_data;\n        wa_q <= {wr_sel, wptr[2:0]};\n    end\n\n    // read address / row-set flag, registered per consumer\n    wire [3:0] rd_addr = {rd_sel, rptr[5:3]};\n    reg  [3:0] ra_q;\n    reg        ro_q;      // 0: even row set (rows 0,2,4,6), 1: odd set\n    reg        vq;\n    reg [2:0]  iq;\n\n    always @(posedge clk) begin\n        ra_q <= rd_addr;\n        ro_q <= rptr[0] ^ rptr[2];\n        vq   <= reading;\n        iq   <= rptr[2:0];\n    end\n\n    // 8 storage arrays: index = lane*2 + subbank\n    genvar gi;\n    generate\n        for (gi = 0; gi < 8; gi = gi + 1) begin : ARR\n            reg              wen_q;\n            reg signed [DW-1:0] mem [0:15];\n            wire             match = (wptr[5:4] == gi[2:1]) && (wptr[3] == gi[0]);\n            wire signed [DW-1:0] rout;\n            always @(posedge clk) begin\n                wen_q <= wr_en && match;\n                if (wen_q)\n                    mem[wa_q] <= wd_q;\n            end\n            assign rout = mem[ra_q];\n        end\n    endgenerate\n\n    // per-lane even/odd sub-bank selection\n    wire signed [DW-1:0] u0 = ARR[0].rout;\n    wire signed [DW-1:0] u1 = ARR[1].rout;\n    wire signed [DW-1:0] u2 = ARR[2].rout;\n    wire signed [DW-1:0] u3 = ARR[3].rout;\n    wire signed [DW-1:0] u4 = ARR[4].rout;\n    wire signed [DW-1:0] u5 = ARR[5].rout;\n    wire signed [DW-1:0] u6 = ARR[6].rout;\n    wire signed [DW-1:0] u7 = ARR[7].rout;\n\n    assign out_data0 = ro_q ? u1 : u0;\n    assign out_data1 = ro_q ? u3 : u2;\n    assign out_data2 = ro_q ? u5 : u4;\n    assign out_data3 = ro_q ? u7 : u6;\n    assign out_valid = vq;\n    assign out_idx   = iq;\n\n    always @(posedge clk) begin\n        if (rst || img_start_i) begin\n            wptr    <= 6'd0;\n            rptr    <= 6'd0;\n            reading <= 1'b0;\n            wr_sel  <= 1'b0;\n            rd_sel  <= 1'b0;\n
