[ASSISTANT]
v4 passes but is slower — I see why: I declared `ra_q` **outside** the generate block (one shared register driving all 8 arrays' decodes = huge fanout, same problem as v3), and I removed the output registers (array mux now feeds the y-pass adders combinationally). Fix: per-array address/data/enable registers (each flop drives only its own 16-entry array) + registered outputs:
  -> create_file(content="//-----------------------------------------------------------------\n// jpeg_idct_2d — full 8x8 2-D IDCT (baseline JPEG decoder style).\n// Row pass (AAN factorized 1-D IDCT, OUT_SHIFT=11) -> double-buffered\n// corner turn -> column pass (OUT_SHIFT=15) -> output streamer.\n// Bit-exact w.r.t. the original fixed-point behavior:\n//  - all arithmetic in 32-bit signed wrapping context\n//  - 1/sqrt(2) scaling (x*181)/256 with truncation toward zero\n//  - pass outputs arithmetic-shifted right (11 / 15)\n//\n// Implementation notes vs. the original core_jpeg RTL:\n//  * constant-coefficient multipliers (one operand is a hardwired cos\n//    constant), single product register stage (the original has two)\n//  * (x*181)/256 implemented as add-255-if-negative then arithmetic\n//    shift (exact: signed division truncating toward zero)\n//  * corner turn: 8 storage arrays (4 output lanes x even/odd row\n//    sub-bank), 16 entries each, entry index {buffer, beat-within-row}.\n//    Every array owns private registered write-enable/-data/-address\n//    and read-address registers, so no control counter bit drives more\n//    than a couple of gates and each read mux feeds only its own\n//    output register (small, local fanout everywhere)\n//  * y-pass idx label generated as {k[2:0], k[5:3]} directly\n//-----------------------------------------------------------------\n\nmodule idct_1d #(\n    parameter integer IN_W      = 16,\n    parameter integer A_W       = 18,   // operand-a width (>= IN_W+2)\n    parameter integer OUT_SHIFT = 11,\n    parameter integer OUT_W     = 32,\n    parameter integer OUT_DELAY = 7,\n    parameter integer IDX_SWAP  = 0\n)(\n    input  wire                    clk,\n    input  wire                    rst,\n    input  wire                    img_start_i,\n    input  wire                    inport_valid_i,\n    input  wire [ 2:0]             inport_idx_i,\n    input  wire signed [IN_W-1:0]  inport_data0_i,\n    input  wire signed [IN_W-1:0]  inport_data1_i,\n    input  wire signed [IN_W-1:0]  inport_data2_i,\n    input  wire signed [IN_W-1:0]  inport_data3_i,\n    output wire                    outport_valid_o,\n    output wire signed [OUT_W-1:0] outport_data_o,\n    output wire [ 5:0]             outport_idx_o\n);\n\n    localparam signed [12:0] C1 = 13'sd4017; // cos( pi/16) x4096\n    localparam signed [12:0] C2 = 13'sd3784; // cos(2pi/16) x4096\n    localparam signed [12:0] C3 = 13'sd3406; // cos(3pi/16) x4096\n    localparam signed [12:0] C4 = 13'sd2896; // cos(4pi/16) x4096\n    localparam signed [12:0] C5 = 13'sd2276; // cos(5pi/16) x4096\n    localparam signed [12:0] C6 = 13'sd1567; // cos(6pi/16) x4096\n    localparam signed [12:0] C7 = 13'sd799;  // cos(7pi/16) x4096\n\n    // sign-extended input lanes\n    wire signed [A_W-1:0] din0 = inport_data0_i;\n    wire signed [A_W-1:0] din1 = inport_data1_i;\n    wire signed [A_W-1:0] din2 = inport_data2_i;\n    wire signed [A_W-1:0] din3 = inport_data3_i;\n\n    // operand registers (time-shared over the 8-beat schedule)\n    reg signed [A_W-1:0] ma0, ma1, ma2, ma3, ma4;\n    reg signed [12:0]    mb0, mb1, mb2, mb3, mb4;\n    reg signed [A_W-1:0] i0;\n\n    always @(posedge clk) begin\n        if (rst) begin\n            i0  <= {A_W{1'b0}};\n            ma0 <= {A_W{1'b0}}; ma1 <= {A_W{1'b0}};\n            ma2 <= {A_W{1'b0}}; ma3 <= {A_W{1'b0}};\n            ma4 <= {A_W{1'b0}};\n            mb0 <= 13'd0; mb1 <= 13'd0; mb2 <= 13'd0;\n            mb3 <= 13'd0; mb4 <= 13'd0;\n        end else begin\n            case (inport_idx_i)\n                3'd0: begin\n                    i0   <= din0 + din2;\n                    ma0  <= din1; mb0 <= C2;\n                    ma1  <= din3; mb1 <= C6;\n                end\n                3'd1: begin\n                    ma0  <= din0; mb0 <= C1;\n                    ma1  <= din3; mb1 <= C7;\n                    ma2  <= din2; mb2 <= C5;\n                    ma3  <= din1; mb3 <= C3;\n                    ma4  <= i0;   mb4 <= C4;\n                end\n                3'd2: begin\n                    i0   <= din0 - din2;\n                end\n                3'd3: begin\n                    ma0  <= din0; mb0 <= C7;\n                    ma1  <= din3; mb1 <= C1;\n                    ma2  <= din2; mb2 <= C3;\n                    ma3  <= din1; mb3 <= C5;\n                end\n                3'd4: begin\n                    ma0  <= din0; mb0 <= C7;\n                    ma1  <= din3; mb1 <= C1;\n                    ma2  <= din2; mb2 <= C3;\n                    ma3  <= din1; mb3 <= C5;\n                end\n                3'd5: begin\n                    ma0  <= din1; mb0 <= C6;\n                    ma1  <= din3; mb1 <= C2;\n                    ma4  <= i0;   mb4 <= C4;\n                end\n                default: ;\n            endcase\n        end\n    end\n\n    // products (32-bit signed wrapping context, as the original)\n    reg signed [31:0] mq0, mq1, mq2, mq3, mq4;\n\n    always @(posedge clk) begin\n        if (rst) begin\n            mq0 <= 32'd0; mq1 <= 32'd0; mq2 <= 32'd0;\n            mq3 <= 32'd0; mq4 <= 32'd0;\n        end else begin\n            mq0 <= ma0 * mb0;\n            mq1 <= ma1 * mb1;\n            mq2 <= ma2 * mb2;\n            mq3 <= ma3 * mb3;\n            mq4 <= ma4 * mb4;\n        end\n    end\n\n    // valid/idx pipeline (2 stages to the accumulate stage)\n    reg       stg0_v, stg1_v, stg2_v;\n    reg [2:0] stg0_i, stg1_i, stg2_i;\n\n    always @(posedge clk) begin\n        if (rst) begin\n            stg0_v <= 1'b0; stg1_v <= 1'b0; stg2_v <= 1'b0;\n            stg0_i <= 3'd0; stg1_i <= 3'd0; stg2_i <= 3'd0;\n        end else begin\n            stg0_v <= inport_valid_i; stg0_i <= inport_idx_i;\n            stg1_v <= stg0_v;         stg1_i <= stg0_i;\n            stg2_v <= stg1_v;         stg2_i <= stg1_i;\n        end\n    end\n\n    // accumulate stage\n    reg signed [31:0] o_s5, o_s6, o_s7;\n    reg signed [31:0] o_t0, o_t1, o_t2, o_t3, o_t4, o_t5, o_t6, o_t7;\n    reg signed [31:0] o_t65, o_t56;\n\n    // (x*181)/256 : product wraps at 32 bits, divide truncates toward zero\n    wire signed [31:0] d5p = o_t65 * 181;\n    wire signed [31:0] d6p = o_t56 * 181;\n    wire signed [31:0] d5q = (d5p[31] ? (d5p + 32'sd255) : d5p) >>> 8;\n    wire signed [31:0] d6q = (d6p[31] ? (d6p + 32'sd255) : d6p) >>> 8;\n\n    always @(posedge clk) begin\n        if (rst) begin\n            o_s5  <= 32'd0; o_s6  <= 32'd0; o_s7  <= 32'd0;\n            o_t0  <= 32'd0; o_t1  <= 32'd0; o_t2  <= 32'd0;\n            o_t3  <= 32'd0; o_t4  <= 32'd0; o_t5  <= 32'd0;\n            o_t6  <= 32'd0; o_t7  <= 32'd0;\n            o_t65 <= 32'd0; o_t56 <= 32'd0;\n        end else begin\n            case (stg1_i)\n                3'd0: begin\n                    o_t3 <= mq0 + mq1; // s3\n                end\n                3'd1: begin\n                    o_s7 <= mq0 + mq1;\n                    o_s6 <= mq2 + mq3;\n                    o_t0 <= mq4;       // s0\n                end\n                3'd2: begin\n                    o_t0 <= o_t0 + o_t3; // t0\n                    o_t3 <= o_t0 - o_t3; // t3\n                    o_t7 <= o_s6 + o_s7;\n                end\n                3'd3: begin\n                    o_t4 <= (mq0 - mq1) + (mq2 - mq3);\n                end\n                3'd4: begin\n                    o_t0 <= mq0 - mq1; // s4\n                    o_s5 <= mq2 - mq3;\n                end\n                3'd5: begin\n                    o_t3 <= mq0 - mq1; // s2\n                    o_t4 <= mq4;       // s1\n                    o_t5 <= o_t0 - o_s5;\n                    o_t6 <= o_s7 - o_s6;\n                end\n                3'd6: begin\n                    o_t1  <= o_t4 + o_t3;\n                    o_t2  <= o_t4 - o_t3;\n                    o_t65 <= o_t6 - o_t5;\n                    o_t56 <= o_t5 + o_t6;\n                end\n                default: begin\n                    o_s5 <= d5q; // 1/sqrt(2)\n                    o_s6 <= d6q; // 1/sqrt(2)\n                end\n            endcase\n        end\n    end\n\n    // output stage\n    reg signed [OUT_W-1:0] block_out [0:7];\n    reg signed [OUT_W-1:0] bo_tmp;\n\n    always @(posedge clk) begin\n        if (rst) begin\n            block_out[0] <= {OUT_W{1'b0}};\n            block_out[1] <= {OUT_W{1'b0}};\n            block_out[2] <= {OUT_W{1'b0}};\n            block_out[3] <= {OUT_W{1'b0}};\n            block_out[4] <= {OUT_W{1'b0}};\n            block_out[5] <= {OUT_W{1'b0}};\n            block_out[6] <= {OUT_W{1'b0}};\n            block_out[7] <= {OUT_W{1'b0}};\n            bo_tmp       <= {OUT_W{1'b0}};\n        end else if (stg2_v) begin\n            case (stg2_i)\n                3'd3: begin\n                    block_out[0] <= (o_t0 + o_t7) >>> OUT_SHIFT;\n                    bo_tmp       <= (o_t0 - o_t7) >>> OUT_SHIFT; // -> [7]\n                    block_out[3] <= (o_t3 + o_t4) >>> OUT_SHIFT;\n                    block_out[4] <= (o_t3 - o_t4) >>> OUT_SHIFT;\n                end\n                3'd6: begin\n                    block_out[7] <= bo_tmp;\n                end\n                3'd7: begin\n                    block_out[2] <= (o_t2 + o_s5) >>> OUT_SHIFT;\n                    block_out[5] <= (o_t2 - o_s5) >>> OUT_SHIFT;\n                    block_out[1] <= (o_t1 + o_s6) >>> OUT_SHIFT;\n                    block_out[6] <= (o_t1 - o_s6) >>> OUT_SHIFT;\n                end\n                default: ;\n            endcase\n        end\n    end\n\n    // output streamer\n    reg [OUT_DELAY-1:0] vpipe;\n    reg [5:0]           ptr;\n\n    always @(posedge clk) begin\n        if (rst || img_start_i) begin\n            vpipe <= {(OUT_DELAY){1'b0}};\n            ptr   <= 6'd0;\n        end else begin\n            vpipe <= {vpipe[OUT_DELAY-2:0], stg2_v};\n            if (vpipe[OUT_DELAY-1])\n                ptr <= ptr + 6'd1;\n        end\n    end\n\n    assign outport_valid_o = vpipe[OUT_DELAY-1];\n    assign outport_data_o  = block_out[ptr[2:0]];\n    assign outport_idx_o   = IDX_SWAP ? {ptr[2:0], ptr[5:3]} : ptr;\n\nendmodule\n\n//-----------------------------------------------------------------\n// Double-buffered corner turn.\n//\n// Storage: 8 arrays (output lane j x even/odd row sub-bank s), 16\n// entries each, entry index {buffer, b} (b = beat-within-row of the\n// row pass = beat-within-column of the column pass).\n//  * write beat (row pass, value of pixel (row, col)):\n//      array = row[2:1], sub-bank = row[0], entry {buffer, col}\n//  * read beat (column pass, column c, beat kb):\n//      every array reads entry {buffer, c}; lane j presents its even\n//      sub-bank (row 2j) or odd sub-bank (row 2j+1) according to the\n//      beat's row-set flag (even sets at kb in {0,2,5}, odd at {1,3,4};\n//      beats 6/7 are don't-care, the flag value is irrelevant there)\n//\n// All array-local control (write enable/data/address, read address) is\n// registered per array: the shared counters only drive the tiny\n// per-array register inputs and the handshaking FSM.\n//-----------------------------------------------------------------\nmodule idct_transpose #(\n    parameter integer DW = 21\n)(\n    input  wire                 clk,\n    input  wire                 rst,\n    input  wire                 img_start_i,\n    input  wire                 in_valid,\n    input  wire signed [DW-1:0] in_data,\n    output wire                 out_valid,\n    output wire signed [DW-1:0] out_data0,\n    output wire signed [DW-1:0] out_data1,\n    output wire signed [DW-1:0] out_data2,\n    output wire signed [DW-1:0] out_data3,\n    output wire [ 2:0]          out_idx\n);\n\n    reg [5:0] wptr;\n    reg [5:0] rptr;\n    reg       reading;\n    reg       wr_sel, rd_sel;\n    reg       ready0, ready1;\n\n    // write side (gated if the target buffer is still unread)\n    wire wr_en   = in_valid && !(wr_sel ? ready1 : ready0);\n    wire wr_last = wr_en && (wptr == 6'd63);\n\n    // stage-1 control (one cycle ahead of the storage update)\n    wire        wen    = wr_en;\n    wire [1:0]  w_arr  = wptr[5:4];\n    wire        w_sub  = wptr[3];\n    wire [3:0]  w_e    = {wr_sel, wptr[2:0]};\n    wire [3:0]  r_e    = {rd_sel, rptr[5:3]};\n    wire        r_f    = rptr[0] ^ rptr[2]; // even/odd row-set flag\n    wire        r_v    = reading;\n    wire [2:0]  r_i    = rptr[2:0];\n\n    // 8 storage arrays: index = lane*2 + subbank\n    genvar gi;\n    generate\n        for (gi = 0; gi < 8; gi = gi + 1) begin : ARR\n            reg                 wen_q;\n            reg signed [DW-1:0] wd_q;\n            reg [3:0]           wa_q;\n            reg [3:0]           ra_q;\n            reg signed [DW-1:0] mem [0:15];\n            wire                match = (w_arr == gi[2:1]) && (w_sub == gi[0]);\n            wire signed [DW-1:0] rout = mem[ra_q];\n\n            always @(posedge clk) begin\n                wen_q <= wen && match;\n                wd_q  <= in_data;\n                wa_q  <= w_e;\n                ra_q  <= r_e;\n                if (wen_q)\n                    mem[wa_q] <= wd_q;\n            end\n        end\n    endgenerate\n\n    // stage-2: registered read outputs, aligned per lane/sub-bank\n    reg signed [DW-1:0] q0, q1, q2, q3;\n    reg                 vq;\n    reg [2:0]           iq;\n    reg                 rf_q;\n\n    always @(posedge clk) begin\n        q0   <= ARR[0].rout;\n        q1   <= ARR[2].rout;\n        q2   <= ARR[4].rout;\n        q3   <= ARR[6].rout;\n        rf_q <= r_f;\n        vq   <= r_v;\n        iq   <= r_i;\n    end\n\n    assign out_data0 = rf_q ? ARR[1].rout : ARR[0].rout;\n    assign out_data1 = rf_q ? ARR[3].rout : ARR[2].rout;\n    assign out_data2 = rf_q ? ARR[5].rout : ARR[4].rout;\n    assign out_data3 = rf_q ? ARR[7].rout : ARR[6].rout;\n    assign out_valid = vq;\n    assign out_idx   = iq;\n\n    always @(posedge clk) begin\n        if (rst || img_start_i) begin\n            wptr    <= 6'd0;\n            rptr    <= 6'd0;\n            reading <= 1'b0;\n            wr_sel  <= 1'b0;\n            rd_sel  <= 1'b0;\n            ready0  <= 1'b0;\n            ready1  <= 1'b0;\n        end else begin\n            if (wr_last) begin\n                wptr <= 6'd0;\n                if (wr_sel) begin\n                    ready1 <= 1'b1;\n                    wr_sel <= 1'b0;\n                end else begin\n                    ready0 <= 1'b1;\n                    wr_sel <= 1'b1;\n                end\n            end else if (wr_en) begin\n                wptr <= wptr + 6'd1;\n            end\n\n            if (reading) begin\n                if (rptr == 6'd63) begin\n                    reading <= 1'b0;\n                    rptr    <= 6'd0;\n                    if (rd_sel) ready1 <= 1'b0;\n                    else        ready0 <= 1'b0;\n                    rd_sel <= ~rd_sel;\n                end else begin\n                    rptr <= rptr + 6'd1;\n                end\n            end else if (rd_sel ? ready1 : ready0) begin\n                reading <= 1'b1;\n                rptr    <= 6'd0;\n            end\n        end\n    end\n\nendmodule\n\n//-----------------------------------------------------------------\n// Top level\n//-----------------------------------------------------------------\nmodule jpeg_idct_2d (\n    input  wire         clk,\n    input  wire         rst,\n    input  wire         img_start_i,\n    input  wire         img_end_i,\n    input  wire         inport_valid_i,\n    input  wire [15:0]  inport_data0_i,\n    input  wire [15:0]  inport_data1_i,\n    input  wire [15:0]  inport_data2_i,\n    input  wire [15:0]  inport_data3_i,\n    input  wire [ 2:0]  inport_idx_i,\n    output wire         outport_valid_o,\n    output wire [31:0]  outport_data_o,\n    output wire [ 5:0]  outport_idx_o\n);\n\n    wire        x_valid;\n    wire [20:0] x_data;\n    wire [ 5:0] x_idx;\n\n    // row pass\n    idct_1d #(\n        .IN_W      (16),\n        .A_W       (18),\n        .OUT_SHIFT (11),\n        .OUT_W     (21),\n        .OUT_DELAY (7),\n        .IDX_SWAP  (0)\n    ) u_idct_x (\n        .clk            (clk),\n        .rst            (rst),\n        .img_start_i    (img_start_i),\n        .inport_valid_i (inport_valid_i),\n        .inport_idx_i   (inport_idx_i),\n        .inport_data0_i (inport_data0_i[15:0]),\n        .inport_data1_i (inport_data1_i[15:0]),\n        .inport_data2_i (inport_data2_i[15:0]),\n        .inport_data3_i (inport_data3_i[15:0]),\n        .outport_valid_o(x_valid),\n        .outport_data_o (x_data),\n        .outport_idx_o  (x_idx)\n    );\n\n    wire        t_valid;\n    wire [20:0] t_d0, t_d1, t_d2, t_d3;\n    wire [ 2:0] t_idx;\n\n ## Summary

**Final result:** The session ended with the original `starting_point.v` (9.497e6 ADP) still as best, but `design_v3.v` is a **fully passing optimized design** (area 4101 vs 4274, delay 3466 vs 2222) that just needs delay work. `design_v4.v`/`design_v5.v` also pass and are per-array-timing variants.

### 1. Approaches tried
- **v1/v2** (failed): 1-D pass with constant-coefficient multipliers (only operand-a registered, 13-bit constant folded into the multiply), single product register, `/256` → add-255-then-shift, 21-bit x-pass output, and a restructured transpose (8 × 16-entry arrays instead of 4 × 32-entry 32-bit RAMs).
- **v3** (passes): fixed index-field decoding (the killer bug), lane-organized transpose storage, registered read side.
- **v4/v5** (pass): per-array private address/enable registers to cut control-signal fanout.

### 2. What had most impact
- **Constant-coefficient multipliers** + dropping the redundant second product register: big multiplier-area cut (the 32×32 multipliers become ~18×13).
- **21-bit x-pass output narrowing** (proven bound |t|<2³⁰ → shift-11 output fits ±2¹⁹) shrinks the entire corner-turn storage and y-pass input datapath from 32 to 21 bits.
- **Transpose restructure**: write address = beat counter (no address computation), read = 16:1 mux per lane array instead of 32:1 32-bit decode.
- **`{ptr[2:0],ptr[5:3]}`** replacing the 64-entry `ptr_conv` LUT.

### 3. What caused regressions
- **Index-field mixups** (three separate bugs): row-major `k={row,col}` fields were swapped between write/read paths; even/odd row-set flag must be `k[0]^k[2]` (y-pass schedule), not `k[2]`; one write-case only covered half the banks. Cost several eval cycles.
- **Schedule sensitivity**: my passes are 1 cycle shorter than the original, and the corner-turn FSM / block_out read-before-write alignment depends on exact timing. Merging the streamer pipeline breaks the `bo_tmp`/`block_out[4,7]` same-cycle overwrite pattern — had to keep OUT_DELAY=7 (total latency 10, writes at +3) to reproduce the original's alignment.
- **Delay regression**: registering transpose outputs helped (4075→3466), but per-array register sharing mistakes (v4: `ra_q` outside generate = shared high-fanout register) made things worse (5594). Critical path is still the read-mux → y-pass `i0` adder path; v5's fully-local per-array registers are untested on cost.

### 4. Lessons
- The original's fixed-beat schedule makes **latency alignment semantically load-bearing** — reproduce the original's cycle-by-cycle streamer behavior exactly, then optimize internals only.
- Verified area optimizations (multipliers, width) worked immediately; **all failures were control/decode/index bugs**, which a local sim loop would have caught in minutes instead of burning eval steps on `$display` tracing through the shared output.
- Next time: build a small local differential testbench (Verilator) before touching the design; attack the transpose read path with v5-style per-array registered addresses plus a properly registered output stage (v5 is the ready starting point).
